## Costa Rica added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Costa Rica's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **25 books, all of them student books: the five CONED distance-secondary antologías *Antología de Matemáticas*, Niveles 7–11 (UNED/CONED — Nivel Sétimo 2017, Nivel Noveno 2018, Nivel 11 2019; Nivel Décimo by MSc. Jorge Díaz Porras) and the ITCR Proyecto EVEPRIM primary books for Grades 3 and 6 (10 textos + 10 folletos de prácticas across Relaciones y Álgebra, Números, Geometría, Medidas, Estadística and Probabilidad); 0 teacher guides for Costa Rica Maths exist in hand** — and placed 229 teaching items. **SET CORRECTION: the expansion map's count of 17 student is WRONG** — 17 is exactly the number of Costa Rica Mathematics rows in textbooks.tsv (10 EVEPRIM textos + only 2 of the 10 EVEPRIM folletos + 5 CONED antologías); the TSV was never updated for 8 folletos staged on 2026-10-25, and the staging books.json files and phase3-push-state.json agree on the true set of 25 (the folletos count student-side, per the map's own stated basis). All 25 were grabbed, verified (byte size + MD5 against books.json; the two Relaciones y Álgebra textos, which have no books.json entry, verified by size + title page as the main-repo files) and read in full — no partial excerpts, no duplicates (the SharePoint folder copies of the six texto books matched the staged sizes byte-for-byte, so they are the same files, cited once) and no unopenable files. The three Grade 3 textos were read in their November 2026 editions, the in-hand editions per books.json; the GitHub release mid-size-books-3 still holds the superseded October editions of those three textos (a record-level flag, reported, not fixed — same books, older exports; only the November editions are cited here). Two chapter promises in the CONED books are NOT teaching and place no item anywhere: CONED 7's Capítulo 4 is titled Estadística y Probabilidad but teaches statistics only (probability appears only in its introductory discussion questions, pp. 209–214), and CONED 10's Capítulo 1 objectives promise plane transformations that no Tema in the chapter teaches (only a recall of Grade 8 homothety). The placements' named exclusions — front matter, indices, bibliographies and answer keys (soluciones); the EVEPRIM answer-key chapters and the review chapters that re-teach already-placed sections; the CONED chapter motto/introduction pages and “desafío matemático” pages; and the videos and web interactives the EVEPRIM books link to — are apparatus and are stocked nowhere. The 5 CONED book-error groups found in the read — CONED 7: the angle table (p. 106) labels a 360° angle “CONVEXO”, the inverse-proportionality example (pp. 201–203) states v·t = 247.5 for a product of 330 and “very close to 3 hours” at 78 km/h for ≈4.23 h with garbled fraction work, the triangle inequality (p. 125) omits “any two” sides, the median answer (p. 238) gives 68 for a median of 67, the worked powers page (p. 64) misaligns its results, and a tiling exercise (p. 35) reads “largo por largo”; CONED 8: the table of contents numbers the equations Tema “Tema 4” while the body heads it “Tema 5” (numbering slip only); CONED 9: the SI prefix table (p. 74) gives exa the symbol “R” (exa is E), the radian-to-degree rule (p. 117) is printed inverted/garbled though its worked examples are correct, and the contents duplicates “Función Cuadrática” for Capítulo 3 Temas 1 and 4 and mislabels Capítulo 4 as “Capítulo III”; CONED 10: the falling-body model (p. 373) prints “12” for ½ (the worked values use ½ correctly), the key (p. 42) duplicates “tangentes” where “secantes” is meant, p. 14 asks for the area of a “circunferencia” (the circle), and the pentagon solution (p. 86) drops its degree symbols; CONED 11: the logarithm table (p. 118) misaligns its examples (it prints log₅ 5 = 2, which belongs to log₅ 25), the wound-model solution (p. 172) shows an unsigned division display before the correct result, and “invectiva” (p. 121) is a typo for inyectiva — are carried corrected on the rows they touch. One further note is carried as the placements carry it: the Medidas G3 answer key's building-height estimate reads 45 dam in the October copy and “4 dam” in the November extraction — treated as a re-export/extraction artefact in the key, not teaching content, and cited nowhere. Costa Rica's books are pupil books, so Costa Rica is COUNTED: it joins this lesson's acceptance count wherever the placements stock an item. CONED printed page = PDF page throughout; EVEPRIM pages are cited by PDF page with the book's section numbers (two exceptions print folios: the EVEPRIM 6 Probabilidad texto's printed folio = PDF page − 6 and the EVEPRIM 3 Medidas texto's = PDF page − 7; citations give PDF pages). Into this lesson the placements stock 5 items (items 42, 110, 114, 127 and 177).

**Book 1:** *Antología de Matemáticas — Nivel Octavo* (student antología) — Costa Rica, Universidad Estatal a Distancia (UNED) / Colegio Nacional de Educación a Distancia (CONED); 10,683,163 bytes, 346 PDF pp. Printed page = PDF page throughout. Pupil book (distance-secondary antología).

Pupil textbook.

Record corrections the read proved, carried exactly: the record's “Matemática 2do grado Parte I student textbook” is in fact the COMPLETE Grade 2 workbook (Cuaderno de trabajo — Matemática 2, ISBN 978-959-13-1854-1) — **no Grade 2 student textbook is in hand**, and Grade 2 rests on its workbook, Programa and Orientaciones; the record's Grade 5 student byte size (36,868,096 B) is a truncated copy — the complete file read is 77,146,019 B / 396 pp; the Grade 11 teacher documents belong to the newer Perfeccionamiento programme and do **not** match the in-hand Grade 11 student book (2005 edition — its limits, derivatives and integrals chapters have no teacher companion in hand, and the programme's trigonometry unit has no chapter in that book), so they are filed as that programme's own teacher evidence, never as evidence for the 2005 book; the MINED portal source URLs now return soft-404 pages — the reads used Wayback captures of those same URLs and the GitHub routes. The placements' named exclusions — exercise sets, chapter exercises, self-evaluation sections, answers appendices, mathematical tables, historical feature essays and front/back matter — are stocked nowhere. Printed pages are the books' own printed folios as the placements cite them (each book's printed-page basis is stated in its book entry). ## Bulgaria added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Bulgaria's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **5 files = 4 works, all pupil textbooks: the Бит и Техника (Bit i Tekhnika) *Математика* for Grades 1, 2 and 3 and for Grade 4 in two part-files (1 част / 2 част), 642 PDF pages in all** — and placed 123 teaching items. **The expansion map's “5 student + 0 teacher” is CORRECT as a file count (5 files, 4 works). Full-book vs sample verdict: NO in-hand Maths file is a sample** — every file's extent matches its own imprint's print-sheet count (Grade 1: 20.5 sheets, 128 lessons to printed p. 158 + 12 appendices; Grade 2: 18 sheets, 112 lessons to printed p. 140; Grade 3: 18 sheets, 112 lessons to printed p. 140; Grade 4: 10 + 11.5 sheets, lessons 1–136), and the 8 proven publisher samples in Bulgaria's library record (Music G6, Foreign Language G6/G7, БЕЛ G10/G11 and Geography G5) contain no Maths book, so the library's sample ruling does not touch this set. All five PDFs are image-only with no usable text layer and were read visually from page renders (cover, imprint, the book's own contents, and body pages for every distinct topic); printed folio = PDF page − 1 in every book, and Grade 4 Part 2 restarts its own pagination (its citations are marked “Part 2”). No Bulgaria Maths book was in the course before this merge — the string “Bulgaria” occurred nowhere under `maths/` — so every placed item is new here. Set boundary: everything else Bulgarian Maths in the records is exact-link / reader only and NOT in hand — Riva Maths G1–G4 PDFs (duplicate slots for the same grades), Klett epub Maths G5/G6 readers, Klett FlipHTML5 Maths G7/G8 (fullness unproven) and Архимед Maths G10/G11 Ourboox PDFs — none is part of this read and none is cited here; if any is later secured and read, Bulgaria reverts to PARTIAL until it is stocked. The placements' named exclusions — each grade's diagnostics, self-checks and consolidation lessons, drills, blitz tournaments, games and projects (other than the Grade 4 data project stocked in the statistics lessons), end-of-unit and year reviews, and the Grade 1 book's 12 cut-out appendices (number cards, shape cut-outs, play money, tangram, clock face, addition wheel, 0–20 ruler) — are apparatus and are stocked nowhere; the topics they practise are counted at the lessons that teach them. **Book-internal flag, carried exactly: the Grade 1 book's introduction (printed p. 4) promises the unit litre, but no lesson teaches it — the contents list no litre lesson and the closing units summary (lesson 127, printed p. 155, read on the body page) lists only hour, euro, eurocent, kilogram and centimetre — so the litre is stocked nowhere, and Bulgaria Grade 1 stands as teaching cm, kg, euro/eurocent and the hour, not the litre.** Book errors proper (wrong mathematics) were found nowhere in the pages read; contents-page slips (e.g. Grade 4 Part 1 listing its angle lessons out of order in the contents) are apparatus slips, not teaching errors, and are counted nowhere. The placements' one NEW LESSON NEEDED topic — calculator use (Grade 4 Part 2, lessons 118–119, item 121) — is stocked in the existing `money-and-time/calculator-use` lesson built by the Sri Lanka expansion merge; no second calculator lesson is built. ## Fiji added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Fiji's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **13 pupil textbooks of the Ministry of Education / Curriculum Development Unit series, one for each of Years 1–13** — and placed 1,012 items. The expansion map's count is exact: staging `books.json` holds exactly 13 Mathematics entries (one per year, all `kind: student`) and `phase3-push-state.json` lists all 13 as pushed; no Maths teacher guide is in hand (the AQEP Maths Guides for Lower Primary exist only as AnyFlip reader copies in the download list — not in hand, not counted). The staged PDFs had been pushed and deleted locally, so each book was re-fetched for the read — from the repo tree (Contents API) where the pusher had stored it as a blob, else from the book's recorded `source_url` (a Wayback Machine capture of the ministry file, Beacon Media, or Google Drive) — and the grabbed copies were deleted after their placements were written. The read's nine flagged claims ride with the set, honestly: (1) `textbooks.tsv` carries only 9 of the 13 rows (Years 1, 2, 3, 4, 7, 10, 11, 12, 13) — Years 5, 6, 8 and 9 are staged and pushed but missing from the TSV; (2) the Year 2 scan duplicates printed p. 57 and printed p. 58 is absent, so the Money unit's Activity 12 is not in hand to read and is cited nowhere; (3) the Year 5 capture has no cover/title leaves — the file opens at the Strand 1 divider and the body runs complete, Strand 1 p. 1 to Strand 5 p. 98, its identity resting on the staged record and the body's own structure; (4) Year 4's folio sequence is irregular — folios 44–48 and 54 never appear and folios 42 and 55 each appear twice — but the leaf count closes exactly (85 printed numbers − 6 absent + 2 duplicated = 81 body leaves), every contents strand and unit is present and no activity breaks mid-stream at the gaps, so the evidence points to the printed book's own folio misprints rather than missing leaves, a judgement the file alone cannot prove; (5) Year 3's scan order is scrambled at the end (contents folio ii and printed pp. 46, 51, 52 sit at PDF pp. 76–80) and one activity page appears twice (folioed 47 and 51 — counted once), with folio tracking accounting for all of printed pp. 1–75; (6) Year 10 exists in two packagings — the staged Beacon Media file (9,099,595 bytes, 202 pp.) is the copy cited; an ECopies capture of the same book is 9,079,205 bytes / 200 pp.; (7) Year 12's first Wayback transfer arrived truncated (11,794,148 bytes, broken cross-reference table) and was discarded — a fresh download delivered the complete book (17,085,310 bytes, 284 pp.), which is what was read; (8) Year 1's contents lists Chance at p. 65 and Data at p. 66 where the folios are 64 and 65 — the body count closes exactly, so nothing is missing and the contents is one out; (9) Year 6's interior copyright and acknowledgement pages misname it the “Year 7” book — a copy-paste error in the book itself; its cover metadata, contents, running heads and body are Year 6 throughout, and it is read and placed as Year 6. Book errors found in the read are taught corrected, the corrected form being what the items teach — among them Year 3's conversion-table lines “1,000 cm = 1 km” (correct: 100,000 cm) and “10,000 mm = 1,000 m” (correct: 10 m), Year 4's banner “30 minutes = 1 hour” against its own body's 60, Year 5's “1 000 millimetres = 1 litre”, Year 9's contents “Common Multitudes” (for Common Multiples) and its (a × b)ᵐ = aᵐ × bⁿ misprint, and Year 1's count-in-twos strip printing 9 where 19 belongs; Year 10 carries its own printed errata list (pp. 201–202), whose corrections are noted in the items. Counting grain: one item per distinct teaching point — a named concept, method, rule or worked skill with its printed page. The placements' named exclusions — strand divider pages; contents and acknowledgement pages; pure exercise sets and worked-example repeats of an item already placed; Year 1's “Checking my number” ten-frame reference page (printed p. 32); Year 3's duplicated capacity page (counted once); Year 4's closing multiplication-table chart (printed p. 86); Year 10's printed errata list (pp. 201–202, apparatus — its corrections ride in the items); Year 12's closing motivational quotes and exam tips (printed pp. 273–275); Year 13's bibliography and back matter — are stocked nowhere. Fiji COUNTS in this lesson's taught-in figure: every Fiji book is a pupil textbook. ## Honduras added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Honduras's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **21 files = 18 distinct works: 9 pupil textbooks (Grades 3–9, Grade 10 Matemática I and Grade 11 Matemática III BCH) and 12 teacher-guide files for Grades 1, 2, 4, 5, 7, 8, 9, 10 and 11, the Grade 8 guide held in two editions (2018 and the 2022 revision) and the Grade 1 and 2 guides each in two scans** — all from the Secretaría de Educación's PROMETAM / UPNFM series produced with JICA support — and placed 335 items (218 pupil-textbook items and 117 teacher-evidence items), plus 3 duplicate-edition records (placements items 241, 242 and 338) that are cited once with their works and carry no separate items. The expansion map's “9 student + 12 teacher” count is right: the staging `books.json` files, the `phase3-push-state.json` pushed set and `textbooks.tsv` agree on exactly these 21 files. There is no Grade 1–2 pupil textbook in the Secretaría's programme (workbook only — SE Manual de Lineamientos), so the Grade 1–2 teaching in hand is the two guides; there is no Grade 3 or Grade 6 teacher guide in hand. Every file was grabbed from its recorded `source_url` (educatrachos.hn / libroshonduras.com) after the staged PDFs had been pushed and deleted locally, and verified by exact byte size against the download-list record and by page count against the `books.json` record before reading; grabbed copies were deleted after each book's text was extracted. Read basis, stated honestly: the six text-layer pupil books and eight text-layer guides were read in full from their text; the three scanned pupil books (Grades 7–9) were read by OCR of their Índices and unit/lesson openers with interior pages OCR-sampled — Grades 7 and 8 cross-checked against the full text-layer read of their own teacher guides, which plan every lesson class by class — and the two 2022 guides are themselves scans, OCR-read at cover, presentation and index level, the Grade 9 guide therefore placed at unit grain. Counting grain: one item per Índice lesson; a lesson teaching two distinct course topics is split into two items, and three items (a Grade 4 pupil lesson, a Grade 10 pupil lesson and a Grade 2 guide unit) each carry a row in two lessons below and are counted once. The placements' named exclusions — the “Páginas para recortar” cut-out pages of the Grades 3, 4 and 5 pupil books (manipulatives, not teaching text), the Grade 6 book's closing “Repaso” review, and the “Nos divertimos” / “¿Sabías que?” enrichment boxes inside placed lessons — are stocked nowhere. The read's flagged claims ride with the set, honestly: (1) the download list §A's original 2026-10-21 mirror labels for the III-Ciclo files were swapped between Español and Matemáticas — the files it calls “Matemáticas 7/8/9 (2018 PROMETAM)” are the Español books, and its “Español 7/8/9” alternates are the Matemáticas books — a library-record fault, already corrected in `books.json`, reported here and not fixed by this merge; (2) the Grade 9 guide's cover misprints its cycle as “II Ciclo” where the programme is III Ciclo; (3) the Grade 11 pupil book's Índice misprints “Cincunferencias” for “Circunferencias”, taught corrected; (4) the 2014 scan of the Grade 1 guide has a materially poorer text layer than the mirror scan of the same work, so anyone text-mining it alone will under-read it. Book errors found in the read are taught corrected, the corrected form being what the items teach. Into this lesson the placements stock 8 items — 4 pupil-textbook rows and 4 teacher-evidence rows — from Book 5 (*Matemática I, Libro del Estudiante, Décimo grado*), Book 11 (*Guía del Docente, Matemática I, Décimo grado*), Book 13 (*Guía del Docente, Matemáticas, 7mo grado (2018)*), Book 14 (*Guía del Docente, Matemáticas, 8vo grado (2018)*), Book 15 (*Libro del Estudiante, Matemáticas, 7mo grado*), Book 16 (*Libro del Estudiante, Matemáticas and 8vo grado*).

## Italy added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Italy's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **14 pupil textbooks, all adopted commercial titles (Italy has no government textbooks; schools adopt titles under the AIE/MIUR adozioni system): the Bergamini *Matematica.blu* volumes (Zanichelli) and the Sasso *Colori della Matematica* Edizione Verde and Edizione Blu volumes and triennio modules A–G (Petrini / De Agostini Scuola)** — and placed 781 items. The expansion map's “14 student + 0 teacher” count and kind split are exact against the staging `books.json` files, `textbooks.tsv` and the push state; every file was verified against its record (byte size, and MD5 where the record carries one) and read in full. Counting grain: one item per numbered theory section of the book's own Indice/Sommario. Two extent faults ride with the set, honestly: the files filed as *Matematica.blu* Vol. 1 (Book 3) and *Matematica.blu 2.0* Vol. 5 (Book 4) are each only the SECOND PART of the volume — Vol. 1 opens at Capitolo 13 (printed p. 670) and its Capitoli 1–12 and G1–G4 are not in the file; Vol. 5 opens at Capitolo 25 (printed p. 1560) and its Capitoli 18–24 are not in the file — so both are named as partial volumes wherever they are cited, only their held content is placed, and the missing first parts are not in hand anywhere and are cited nowhere (the ground the Vol. 1 first part would have covered is supplied inside this set by Books 5–6 at Sasso depth, and the Vol. 5 first part's limits and continuity ground by Book 12, Modulo G). The placements' named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the eBook-only units the books' own Indici mark “(solo nell'eBook)”; and the apparatus around the numbered theory sections in every book — chapter openings, exercise blocks, laboratories, *Realtà e modelli*, exam-preparation sections (*Verso l'esame*, *Verso l'INVALSI*, *Verso l'università*), glossaries, answer keys, formula sheets, and *Approfondimento* boxes that are not numbered theory sections. Into this lesson the placements stock 32 items, every one a pupil-textbook row, from Book 1 (*Matematica.blu — Vol. 2*), Book 3 (*Matematica.blu — Vol. 1*, second part only), Book 5 (*Colori della Matematica — Edizione Verde, Vol. 1*), Book 6 (*Colori della Matematica — Edizione Blu, Vol. 1*), Book 7 (*Colori della Matematica — Edizione Blu, Vol. 2*) and Book 13 (*Colori della Matematica — Edizione Blu, Modulo A*).

## Mauritius added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Mauritius's whole in-hand Maths-content set from the files — **2 documents, and no pupil textbook among them**: (A) the *Numeracy — Grade 7, Part 1, Pupil's Worksheets* of the Foundation Programme in Literacy, Numeracy and Skills (© Mauritius Institute of Education 2025) — pupil worksheets, but only a **partial excerpt**: the file holds printed pp. 1–5, the first topic of Part 1 (“Let's Measure in practical situations”, length only), ending inside that topic's exercise set; and (B) the *Extended Programme Progress Book, Grades 7–9+* (Ministry of Education, Tertiary Education, Science and Technology), a record-of-progress book filed in the library records under the subject “Assessment”, whose Mathematics sections (printed pp. 25–30 for Grades 7–8, printed pp. 105–124 for Grades 9–9+) state the competencies a pupil must reach — **curriculum evidence only**: the Progress Book teaches nothing itself, and every Progress Book row below is worded as the requirement it is and is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure. The expansion map is WRONG in substance for Mauritius (flag F2): it recorded “1 student / 0 teacher” in hand, but the counted file is a supplementary worksheets excerpt, not a pupil textbook, and the Progress Book was not counted at all; the true in-hand Maths-content set is 0 pupil textbooks, 0 teacher guides, 1 pupil worksheets excerpt and 1 progress-record book. Flag F1: the GitHub record marks the worksheets `quarters: "full"` and the file is named `pupil-full-year.pdf`, but the file itself is 7 PDF pages carrying printed pp. 1–5 only — whatever else Part 1 contains is not in hand, and the excerpt is cited only for the pages actually held (both flags are record-level; the records themselves are not edited — merges never edit the records). All other Mauritius Maths books — the MIE primary and secondary Mathematics textbooks (exact links only) and Prevocational Mathematics Year 2 (ministry link only, extra-grid) — are link-only, not in hand and not read; the other in-hand Mauritius files (Road Safety Education Grades 1–6, *A Pictorial Primer — Say No to Tobacco*, Kreol Morisien textbooks, the Pre-Primary curriculum documents) are other subjects, verified by a full census of the Mauritius folders. The placements' named exclusions — Book A's front matter (cover, Numeracy Panel page, copyright); the Grade 7 colour-naming competency (no mathematics content as stated; the pattern competency it prepares for is counted at Grade 8); the Grade 9 “perform arithmetic operations mentally” process statement; the incomplete printed fragment “including algebraic fractions ( )” at Grade 9+; the Progress Book's non-Mathematics subject sections and its blank record apparatus; and record items R1–R3 (a Physical Education cross-reference, the front-matter GPA worked example, and the worksheets cover's external game link) — are stocked nowhere. The Progress Book's book errors are carried corrected on the rows they touch: the Grade 7 fraction list prints ½ twice (item 17); the H.C.F./L.C.M. word-problem requirement is stated a second time, misplaced inside §1.6 Decimals (item 94); three statements are truncated in the book — “involving brackets of the form”, the form never given (item 145), “a^n for n = −1, −2, −3 and rational” (item 153), and “or in the form” (item 216); §5.2 is headed “Pictograms and bar charts” but states only frequency-table work (item 151); π is printed as the Cyrillic letter “л” (item 172); and the quadratic rows are printed under the §3.5 Matrices heading with the general form garbled as “( a 2+b +c)”, read as ax² + bx + c (items 199–202). Mauritius's class for Maths is **PARTIAL (evidence-heavy)**: only the 10 taught items rest on pupil material, and that from a 5-page excerpt, so Mauritius is named in the countries line and in the rows below but is added to NO lesson's acceptance count, on the Bahamas/Canada evidence precedent. Into this lesson the placements stock 3 curriculum-evidence items from Book B.

**Book B:** *Extended Programme Progress Book, Grades 7–9+* (progress record book) — Mauritius, Ministry of Education, Tertiary Education, Science and Technology (Mauritius Institute of Education property per its copyright notice); 1,354,101 bytes, 218 PDF pages, MD5 aff08e158c2ad9ee08dd1815251052be. Filed in the records under subject “Assessment” (kind workbook). Complete as published; only its Mathematics sections are placed here (Grades 7–8: printed pp. 25–30; Grades 9–9+: printed pp. 105–124). Printed folio = PDF page. CURRICULUM EVIDENCE ONLY — the book states the competencies a pupil must reach and records progress against them; it teaches nothing itself, and its rows are counted in no lesson's taught-in figure.

## Iceland added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Iceland's whole in-hand Maths set from the files — **28 files: 14 pupil books and 14 teacher books**, all published by MMS (Miðstöð menntunar og skólaþjónustu, formerly Námsgagnastofnun), the Icelandic state publisher; the Sproti (Grades 1–4) and Stika (Grades 5–7) series are Icelandic adaptations of the Norwegian *Multi* series (Gyldendal), and Skali is the Icelandic adaptation of the Norwegian *Maximum* series — and placed 229 items (161 pupil + 68 teacher evidence). The expansion map's count and kind split are exact. Three record matters ride with the set (record-level; the records themselves are not edited — merges never edit the records): textbooks.tsv omits the Stika 1a Kennarabók (27 TSV rows for the true 28 files — the book itself is in hand, in books.json, the push-state and the GitHub release); no Stika 1b Kennarabók exists in any record, although the Stika 1a pupil book's own front matter lists kennarabækur 1a and 1b as series components, so Stika 1b pupil content stands on the pupil book alone; and Sproti 4b's secured PDF (154 pp.) files its Ch. 9 (Mælingar og tugabrot) twice — PDF pp. 50–71 and PDF pp. 72–93 are the same printed chapter — counted once in every citation below. The set holds only the Skali 1A teacher book for Grade 8 — no Skali pupil book is in hand — so every Skali item is teacher evidence. The placements' named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the workbooks (æfingahefti) and worksheet files, which exist only as download-list links and are not in the in-hand set; the Skali pupil books (1A/1B nemendabækur), not in hand; the other MMS series in the download list (Stærðfræði 8+/9+/10+, Vinkill, Áttatíu), not in hand and not read; and front matter, the books' SPIL game pages as standalone content, and the Norwegian-original credits, none counted as items (the pupil books' own built-in practice pages and tests are apparatus inside the books read). Pupil items are COUNTED normally; teacher-evidence items (the 14 kennarabækur) are named in the rows and stay outside the acceptance count. Into this lesson the placements stock 1 pupil items and 1 teacher-evidence item from Book 1 and Book 2.

**Book 1:** *Stika 2b — Nemendabók* (Grade 6 pupil book) — Iceland, MMS (Miðstöð menntunar og skólaþjónustu, formerly Námsgagnastofnun), the Icelandic state publisher; Icelandic adaptation of the Norwegian *Multi* series (Gyldendal); 35,773,231 bytes, 132 PDF pp. Printed p. = PDF p. − 2. PUPIL textbook — counted.

**Book 2:** *Stika 3b — Kennarabók* (Grade 7 teacher book) — Iceland, MMS (Miðstöð menntunar og skólaþjónustu, formerly Námsgagnastofnun), the Icelandic state publisher; Icelandic adaptation of the Norwegian *Multi* series (Gyldendal); 47,562,060 bytes, 140 PDF pp. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY — the kennarabók reproduces the pupil pages with chapter goals, teaching notes, extra activities and answers; counted in no pupil total.

| Balance equations: all boxes in a problem weigh the same — find one box's weight (early equation solving) | Iceland — *Stika 2b — Nemendabók* (Grade 6 pupil book) — printed pp. 14–15 |
| Stika 3b Kennarabók gives guidance and answers for Ch. 8 Patterns and algebra — formulas and equations with x | Iceland — *Stika 3b — Kennarabók* (Grade 7 teacher book) — teacher evidence |

Its 1 teacher-evidence item in this lesson is named in the rows and stays outside the count. Iceland's class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED** — every in-hand Iceland Maths file (28 of 28) has been read in full and stocked.

## Myanmar added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Myanmar's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **2 documents, the Ministry of Education *Teacher Guide Mathematics-1, Grade 6* and *Teacher Guide Mathematics-2, Grade 6* (Basic Education Curriculum, Syllabus and Textbook Committee, 2019-2020; Mathematics-1: 133 PDF pages; Mathematics-2: 99 PDF pages); 0 pupil (student) textbooks** — and placed 114 teaching items. Each guide carries the full lesson plans for its half of the Grade 6 year — learning outcomes, lesson objectives, the taught subject matter with worked examples and answers, practice and review; Mathematics-1 covers the number / algebra / statistics / social-mathematics half of the year (Chapters 1–9) and Mathematics-2 the geometry / mensuration half (Chapters 1–10); the two files are two halves of one complete Grade 6 teacher-guide slot, not duplicates of each other and not parts of one file. Every Myanmar item in this section is therefore TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE — the guides teach teachers how to teach the topic; neither book is a pupil textbook — cited as the evidence kind it is; Myanmar is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure, on the standing Ghana evidence-only precedent, until a Myanmar pupil book is in hand and read. No pupil Maths book is in hand: both guides' lesson plans follow a matching Grade 6 pupil textbook (the guides refer throughout to “the textbook”, its figures, examples and exercises — e.g. Mathematics-1 exercises 1.1–9.8); that pupil textbook exists but is not in hand (placements record item R1) — the guides' references to it are apparatus, not pupil content read here — and every Myanmar student Maths book the library records locate is an exact-link record only, never downloaded; none is part of this read and none is cited. The expansion map's count and kind split are exact (0 student + 2 teacher): textbooks.tsv carries exactly 8 Myanmar rows, all Grade 6 teacher guides, exactly two of them Mathematics; neither push-state names Myanmar (the books were secured pre-phase-3 into the textbooks repo's main tree) and phase3-staging holds no Myanmar directory, so the read copies were grabbed by the authenticated GitHub API route at the exact recorded byte sizes (Mathematics-1: 1,117,915 bytes; Mathematics-2: 1,312,022 bytes) and deleted after the read. Printed page = PDF page throughout both guides (the folio printed at the head of every page equals its PDF page). The placements' counting grain is one item per distinct taught content stated in a lesson plan's objective and teaching section, splitting a lesson where it teaches two contents that belong to different course lessons; the guides' shared pedagogy apparatus (student-centred method, Bloom's taxonomy, assessment, the I-T-P-R lesson stages, per-lesson introduction/practice/review conduct) is not counted per lesson, and exercise sets drawn from the matching pupil textbook are apparatus serving the lesson's own item, not separate content. The placements' named exclusions — the shared pedagogy Introduction in both guides (printed pp. 3–14 of each: student-centred method, basic rules for learning activity, Bloom's taxonomy, questioning stages, classroom assessment, 21st-century/soft skills and classroom social aspects; the syllabus tables inside it are placements record item R2 — the programme frame for the lesson plans, not separate taught content, and no syllabus-only topic was found that no lesson plan teaches), the I-T-P-R lesson-stage apparatus, entry-exit checks, group-work conduct and learning-journal directions repeated in every lesson plan, the exercise numbers cited from the pupil textbook (a book not in hand), and the glossary both prefaces announce, which is carried in the textbook and stands in neither guide as printed (recorded as an absence, verified on the read) — are stocked nowhere. The placements' record items R3–R4 (Mathematics-1's contents skips section 1.4 in its numbering although the body teaches 1.4 Distributive Property at printed pp. 26–27, and lists section 5.3 twice for the body's two successive 5.3 lessons, placed once as item 49; Mathematics-2's contents heads Chapter 9 without its chapter-number line although the body heads it “Chapter 9 Mensuration (1)” at printed p. 79) are contents slips, stocked nowhere. The placements' book errors E1–E8 are taught corrected, each noted on the row it touches. Into this lesson the placements stock 4 teacher-guide-evidence items from Book 1.

**Book 1:** *Teacher Guide Mathematics-1, Grade 6* (teacher guide, Grade 6) — Myanmar, Ministry of Education (The Republic of the Union of Myanmar), Basic Education Curriculum, Syllabus and Textbook Committee, 2019-2020; 1,117,915 bytes, 133 PDF pages. Printed page = PDF page throughout. TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE ONLY — the guide teaches the teacher how to teach the topic; it is not a pupil textbook. Covers Chapters 1–9, the number / algebra / statistics / social-mathematics half of the Grade 6 year.

## Mozambique added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Mozambique’s whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **15 files: 14 pupil Matemática modules of the ministry’s PESD distance-learning programme (PESD I Módulos 1–5, studied by the 1º ciclo = grades 7–9; PESD II Módulos 1–9, studied by the 2º ciclo = grades 10–12) and 1 teacher guide, the *Manual do Professor — 1.ª Classe*, whose Matemática part (printed pp. 146–183) is teacher evidence** — all from the Ministério da Educação e Desenvolvimento Humano (MINEDH) through its Instituto de Educação Aberta e à Distância (IEDA) — and placed 233 items (227 pupil-textbook items and 6 teacher-evidence items). Mozambique’s basis, by the user’s 2026-11-02 ruling, is this ministry distance-learning (PESD) set, not the commercial print set: the modules are studied by cycle, not per grade, and are cited as distance-learning books wherever they appear. The expansion map’s “14 student” count is exact, but its “0 teacher” is wrong — the teacher guide is filed under subject “general” at grade-01 and carries a full Matemática part; the true in-hand set is 14 pupil + 1 teacher guide. The three staging `books.json` files, `textbooks.tsv` and `phase3-push-state.json` agree on exactly these 15 files; no staging PDF remained on local disk (pusher-secured, local copies deleted), so every file was fetched fresh from its recorded official source, ieda.gov.mz, and matched its recorded byte size exactly; all 15 MD5s are distinct — no duplicates — and the generically named PESD II file `Modulo-9.pdf` is proved by its title page to be Matemática Módulo 9. Printed-page basis: PESD I modules — printed page = PDF page − 1 (footer-verified); PESD II modules — printed page = the contents-page foliation; the *Manual do Professor* — printed page = PDF page. Counting grain: one item per lesson of a module where the lesson teaches one course topic; a lesson is split into two items where it teaches two clearly distinct course topics. Book errors found on the read are taught corrected, as the placements record them: PESD II Módulo 3’s cover subtitle wrongly repeats Módulo 1’s (“Conjuntos Numéricos e Cálculo Algébrico”) — its interior is exponential and logarithmic functions throughout — and its answer section is headed “Soluções Modulo 1” (sic) although it is Módulo 3’s own key; PESD II Módulo 9’s answer section is headed “Soluções Módulo 8” (sic) although it is Módulo 9’s own key; PESD II Módulo 5’s contents numbers two different lessons “Lição 4” (the linear-function revision, printed p. 43, and the modulus function, printed p. 55) — both lessons are placed, as items 177 and 178 of this read; and PESD I Módulo 4’s contents page omits its Lição 13, which exists in the body at printed p. 64 and is placed (item 96), while its Lições 14 and 15 carry identical stated objectives and are placed as two items as printed (items 97 and 98). The placements’ record items are apparatus, stocked nowhere: R1 — every PESD module’s closing preparation test (teste de preparação), answer key (chave de correcção / soluções) and bibliography; R2 — the *Manual do Professor*’s answer key to the 1.ª Classe pupil book (printed pp. 174–183; the pupil book itself is not in hand); R3 — the DGD Módulo 1 spot-check copy, fetched only to verify its subject identity. The placements’ named exclusions are stocked nowhere: DGD (Desenho e Geometria Descritiva, PESD II Módulos 1, 3–12 — a separate named subject of the Mozambican curriculum with its own module series, interior-checked on Módulo 1 and excluded as such; Módulo 2 is not in hand at all); all other PESD subjects in hand (Biologia, Química, Física, Geografia, História, Inglês, Português, Francês, TIC, Agro-pecuária, Empreendedorismo, Educação Visual, Filosofia) — separate curriculum subjects, none a Matemática book in disguise; the *Manual do Professor*’s Língua Portuguesa and Educação Física parts (printed pp. 4–145 and 184–215); and the primary print-programme Matemática books (1ª–6ª, Edição revista 2025), which are link-only, not in hand and not read. Pupil items are COUNTED normally; the teacher-evidence items (T1–T6, from the one teacher guide) are named in the rows and stay outside the acceptance count. Into this lesson the placements stock 8 items — 8 pupil-textbook rows — from Book 2 (*Matemática — Módulo 2 (PESD I)*) and Book 7 (*Matemática — Módulo 2 (PESD II)*).

## Nauru added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Nauru's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **10 documents, all of them Learning Through Doing (LTD) *Mathematics Planning Documents*, Nauru editions (2026) — Prep and Years 1–8, plus the LTD *Scope and Sequence, Years 1–8* — teacher planning alignments that sequence an Australian commercial subscription lesson system against the Nauru Mathematics Syllabus, every lesson keyed to a Nauru curriculum code (NRU-*); 0 pupil (student) textbooks, 0 teacher guides, 0 workbooks** — and placed 238 items, every one of them PLANNING/CURRICULUM EVIDENCE: the Nauru syllabus's own key-idea statements as printed on each document's closing “Nauru Curriculum Alignment” pages. Nauru publishes no government textbooks (country record, download list §§F–Q, seven fronts), so Nauru's standing in the course is the syllabus-evidence reckoning: Nauru is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure and is named in the countries line and in the rows below only, on the standing evidence-only precedent (Bahamas / Canada / Myanmar). The expansion map's count is right in number and wrong in kind: it carries Nauru as “10 student Maths books”, but every one of the 10 books.json entries is kind `planning-document` and states in its own approval note that it is NOT a Nauru government student textbook (diff fault F1 — a record-side fault, reported, not fixed by this merge). textbooks.tsv carries 12 Nauru rows, all of them USP IOE ESD items, and zero Mathematics rows — the 10 planning documents live only in books.json and push-state. Australia overlap is ZERO: the Australia read's nine Cambridge/Jacaranda Years 8–10 pupil textbooks share no title, ISBN, byte size or file with this set, and the QCE senior titles cross-referenced in Nauru's country record are link-only, in hand for neither country and read by neither reader; nothing from the Australia placements is cited here. All 10 files were read from local disk in phase3-staging, byte-exact against their books.json records (334,327–887,725 bytes; 67 PDF pages in total; 10 distinct MD5s, no duplicates inside the set); nothing was downloaded. Printed-page basis: the documents carry no printed folios (the Scope's footer “Page N” equals its PDF page), so pages cited are document (PDF) pages — Prep key ideas on alignment pp. 6–7 of 7, Years 1–8 on alignment p. 6 of 6; the term overviews (p. 1) and weekly plans (pp. 2–5) sequence the same key ideas and add none of their own. Counting grain: one item per NRU key idea. The *Scope and Sequence* (887,725 bytes, 13 PDF pages) was read in full and cross-checked — every Scope topic traces to a year document's lessons under the codes placed — so it contributes 0 items of its own; counting it again would double-count one programme as two sources. Book errors found on the read are placed corrected, as the placements record them (diff fault F2): Year 3 NRU-M3.3 prints “suing” for “using” and NRU-G3.3 prints “jey” for “key”; Year 5 NRU-CD5.4 prints “recoding sheets” for “recording sheets”; Year 8 NRU-PA8.4 prints “co-ordiinates”; the Year 8 alignment page prints its first geometry code as “NRU-G9.1” — a Year 9 code inside the Year 8 document — while the Year 8 weekly plans cite the same lessons as NRU-G8.1, and item 226 is placed as the Year 8 item it is; the Year 2 document's Term 4 plan cites “NRU-N3.4” against Year 2 strategy-review lessons, where the alignment page's NRU-N2.4 governs; several weekly-plan code strings are garbled in print — the alignment pages' code lists are clean and govern throughout. The placements also record a document inconsistency (diff fault F3): the Scope's fractions table opens its halves entry at the earliest (Prep) level, but the Prep planning document contains no fractions anywhere — fractions enter the year documents at Year 1 (NRU-N1.8) — and the placements follow the year documents. Some key ideas are explicitly Nauru-localised (Year 3 NRU-G3.3: simple maps of Nauru; Year 6 NRU-CD6.4: data contexts “familiar to Nauruan students”; Year 8 NRU-PA8.6: graphs “using data from Nauru”). The placements' named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the Nauru Mathematics Syllabus aims paragraph printed in every year document (general aims, no subject content); the term overviews and weekly plans as separate items (they sequence the same key ideas, and the LTD lesson titles are the Australian system's commercial catalogue, delivered by subscription — the planning documents do not reproduce the lessons, so no lesson content is placed, only the syllabus key ideas the lessons are sequenced against); “Assessment Opportunities” headers and blank assessment columns (apparatus); the Scope & Sequence's duplicated topic statements across years (one programme, counted once at key-idea grain); the 12 USP IOE ESD files filed under Nauru (not Mathematics, excluded by subject); and the OER4Pacific COL open-schooling Mathematics 11/12 PDFs named in the download list §G (regional OER, never staged under Nauru, not in hand, not read). Into this lesson the placements stock 5 planning/curriculum-evidence items from Book 1 (*LTD Mathematics Planning Document, Year 3, Nauru edition (2026)*), Book 2 (*LTD Mathematics Planning Document, Year 5, Nauru edition (2026)*), Book 3 (*LTD Mathematics Planning Document, Year 6, Nauru edition (2026)*), Book 4 (*LTD Mathematics Planning Document, Year 8, Nauru edition (2026)*).

## Nigeria added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Nigeria’s whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **11 files: 1 pupil textbook and 10 teacher guides** — and placed 199 items (20 pupil-teaching items from the pupil textbook and 179 teacher-evidence items from the ten guides). The pupil textbook is *New Concept Mathematics for Senior Secondary Schools 1* (3rd ed. 2016, Learn Africa, 352 pp., 19,071,603 B), held as a release asset in `mid-size-books-8`; the guides are 7 Learn Africa guides (New Method Primary 1; New Concept JSS 1–3 and SS 1–3) and 3 Cambridge *Excellence in Mathematics* teacher’s guides (Books 1–3). The expansion map’s kind split (0 student + 10 teacher + 1 link-only) is wrong on the student count — the SS1 pupil textbook is in hand, and the map’s “1 link-only” is plausibly this same book misclassified; the true split is 1 student + 10 teacher, 11 files (flag F1, record-side). Record discrepancy (flag F2, record-side): the records describe the held JSS 1 teacher’s guide as a 2024 file of 396,871 B (4th edition), but the file in hand and read is the **2026 fifth edition** (362,534 B, ISBN 978 978 925 9755); the 2024 file is not in hand. Book errors found on the read are taught corrected (flag F3): the JSS 1 guide’s Ch20 states “1 hour = 1°” on the clock face — 1 hour = 30°; the JSS 2 guide prints “99 300 = 9.03 × 10⁴” (should be 9.93 × 10⁴), works a simple-interest example stated with T = 4 years at 3 years, and gives the Pythagorean triple “(5, 12, 23)” (should be (5, 12, 13)); the SS 1 guide prints the sector-area formula as θ/360 × 2πr² (should be θ/360 × πr²) and describes standard-form A as “between 0 and 9” (should be 1 ≤ A < 10); the SS 2 guide works log 0.000 592 and prints the characteristic without its negative/bar sign, and conflates “complementary” with “independent” events in places; the SS 3 guide calls A⁻¹ “the negative matrix” in one section (it is the inverse matrix, used correctly later), with several standard-integral lines and one index law garbled in print. The Primary 1 guide’s printed Contents page numbers do not match its body folios (flag F4) — its items cite body folios throughout. Printed-page basis: Learn Africa guide items cite the guide’s own printed (folio) pages — chapter starts from each book’s contents, verified against body folios; pupil-book items cite the pupil book’s printed pages; Cambridge items cite the guide’s printed topic-start page, each topic’s Student’s Book page span being given in the book’s scheme of work. Counting grain: one item per teaching chapter (Learn Africa books) or per teaching topic (Cambridge guides). ## Jamaica added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Jamaica's whole in-hand Maths set from the files — **8 documents: 7 National Standards Curriculum (NSC) guides that carry a Mathematics section (Grades 1, 2 and 3 Integrated Studies / Language Arts / Mathematics; Grades 4, 5 and 6 Exploratory Core; and the Grades 7–9 Mathematics guide — all Ministry of Education, Youth and Information, © 2018, the Grade 6 guide in its FINAL edition of Jan 08 2020), plus 1 pupil reader** — and placed 311 items: 308 curriculum-evidence items from the guides and 3 pupil-text items from the reader. **Kind split: 0 pupil textbooks, 0 teacher guides, 7 curriculum guides.** A curriculum guide states what is REQUIRED to be taught and how to teach it; it is not a pupil textbook, so every guide item below is CURRICULUM EVIDENCE, worded “curriculum requires”, and Jamaica is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure. The expansion map is wrong on both count and kind (flag F1, record-side, reported not fixed): it listed Jamaica as FILES-IN-HAND with 4 student / 0 teacher Maths books — its build matched the subject field only, counting the three Grades 1–3 guides and the Grades 7–9 guide and missing the three Grades 4–6 Exploratory Core guides, whose subject field reads only “Exploratory Core” while their title pages name Mathematics as one of the four core subjects; and the four files it did count are curriculum guides, not student books. Two further record faults ride with the set (record-side; the records themselves are not edited — merges never edit the records, and no maths/books/jamaica.md filing exists or was created): textbooks.tsv has **no row at all for the Grades 7–9 Mathematics guide** (flag F2), although the file is in staging, in books.json and in the pushed set; and the TSV's Grade 5 row points at a Telegram link instead of the file's secured GitHub asset (flag F3) — the book was read byte-exact from that asset. One file defect, stated plainly: the Grade 5 guide's body font has a broken ToUnicode map, so its text layer extracts as bullet glyphs; its Mathematics section was read in full from rendered page images (120-DPI renders of PDF pp. 139–255 = printed pp. 122–238), every page legible, and it is counted as read on that basis. Printed-page basis: each guide prints its own folio; the Mathematics windows run printed = PDF − 5 (Grade 1), − 33 (Grade 2), − 16 (Grade 3), − 11 (Grade 4), − 17 (Grade 5) and − 12 (Grade 6), and Book 7's pages are its own per-grade section pages. Counting grain: Grades 1–3, one item per distinct numbered objective in the Mathematics Content Standards, plus NEW/Extended objectives and Scope-and-Sequence topics stated in no standard (marked (S&S)); Grades 4–6 and 7–9, one item per distinct objective in each Mathematics unit's objectives list; an objective restated verbatim is counted once, at its first full statement. Boundary files examined and excluded — stocked nowhere: the nursery *Birth to Three is Key* early-childhood guide (ages 0–3, all-domains; number concepts are one activity focus, with no Mathematics section or objectives at NSC grain — not a Maths book); the Grades 4–6 Resource & Technology guide (its file is a bundle whose opening leaves are the Grades 7–9 English guide; no Mathematics section); and the Civics (×2), English Language, Integrated Science and Social Studies guides (no Mathematics subject in any of them; the Science guide's measurement/graphing is science content). The placements' named exclusions inside the books are likewise stocked nowhere: the Grade 1 window's “Maths Prayers” (printed pp. 242–243 — devotional poems, religious apparatus, no mathematics content); the Scope and Sequence charts, Process Strand checklists, Illustrative Activities, Benchmarks and Illustrative Examples as apparatus (topics stated only there are placed as items and marked (S&S)); the Integrated Studies and Language Arts windows of the Grades 1–3 guides, which restate the same Mathematics attainment targets as delivery vehicles and state no requirement beyond the window's standards; the Grade 1 book's Mathematics lesson plans and “Teaching Mathematics: Ideas & Activities” (printed pp. 340–400, teacher apparatus); Book 7's Appendices A–E and closing sample lesson plan (printed pp. 191–206, activity apparatus and worked examples of the same objectives); and the reader's copyright/credits leaves. Book errors and faults are carried as the placements record them, taught corrected where they touch a row: the Grade 1 fractions standard (printed p. 275) cross-refers an objective as “Objective 2.3.27” — a Grade 2 bank number inside the Grade 1 book — and objective numbers with a leading “2.” recur throughout the Grade 1 standards (the bank numbering is shared across grades; each such objective is placed once, as stated for Grade 1); the Grade 2 addition example on printed p. 268 (“Pat has 43 oranges … How many apples Pat has now?”) mixes fruits mid-problem — a wording slip, the objective it illustrates placed as read; two Grade 2 standards pages carry a mojibake line (a Caesar-shifted “See previous page for Attainment Targets and Objectives”) — a print-production artefact, no content lost; the Grade 3 window and its Scope and Sequence state **no decimals** anywhere, so no Grade 3 decimals row is stocked; the Grade 4 Term 3 Unit 2 outcome names “cross-sections” of cubes/cuboids, which the unit nowhere teaches — placed inside its item as named-only; the Grade 4 probability unit's header prints “GRAGE 4” (sic); the Grade 5 Overview disagrees with its own units three times, recorded as printed (division of five-digit numbers sits in Term 3 Unit 1, not Term 2; the Overview's Volume has no objective anywhere in the Grade 5 Mathematics section and is stocked nowhere; lines of symmetry sit in Term 2 Unit 3, while the Term 3 Geometry unit is circles, polygons to eight sides, and pyramids); the Grade 6 Term 3 Unit 1 tyre-pressure activity is Science-linked and its arithmetic is already inside its unit's item, not placed separately; in Book 7, the Grade 9 ratio unit's footer prints “NSC Mathematics: Grade 7” (a template error — the unit sits in the Grade 9 section and is placed as Grade 9), the matrices unit header prints “GRADE 9 TERM 1: NUMBERS” although the unit sits in the Term 3 sequence (placed as Term 3), the Grade 7 constructions unit's objectives page repeats the transformations unit's first objective (counted once, in the transformations item), and the Grade 8 Term 2 geometry unit carries a header title copied from the algebra unit while its objectives are polygons and angle sums (placed by its objectives). | Grade 7 Term 2 Algebra curriculum requires formulating rules from knowledge of arithmetic properties; defining and identifying variables, terms, constant, coefficient, expression, equation and operations; identifying, adding and subtracting like and unlike terms; using symbols to represent quantities which vary; writing equations to illustrate word problems; solving simple linear equations using flow diagrams and the balancing method; and simplifying algebraic expressions by grouping like terms | Jamaica — *Grades 7–9 NSC Guide: Mathematics* (curriculum guide) — curriculum evidence, printed p. 37 |
| Grade 8 Term 1 Algebra curriculum requires changing the subject of a simple formula, writing inequalities to illustrate word problems, illustrating inequalities on a number line, and solving simple linear inequalities and representing the solution on a number line | Jamaica — *Grades 7–9 NSC Guide: Mathematics* (curriculum guide) — curriculum evidence, printed p. 69 |

Jamaica enters this lesson as curriculum evidence only: it is named in the countries line and in the rows above, but it is NOT added to the acceptance count above — Jamaica's in-hand Maths set holds no pupil textbook and no teacher guide, only the seven National Standards Curriculum guides, which state what must be taught without being pupil teaching material themselves (the standing evidence-only precedent). Jamaica's class for Maths is PARTIAL (evidence-only): every in-hand document has been read in full and stocked, and it advances only when a Jamaica pupil Maths textbook lands in hand and is read.

Placements: liqlearns-courses-work/expansion-2026-11-10/maths/jamaica-placements.md.

## Serbia added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Serbia's whole in-hand Maths set from the file — **one pupil textbook**: *Математика 1* (Matematika 1 — Mathematics for the first grade of primary school), Нова школа (Nova škola), Belgrade, first edition 2018 — and placed **95 teaching items** from it. The book is a genuine, complete pupil textbook (уџбеник), not a workbook, not an exam paper and not an excerpt: every topic is presented, named and worked before it is practised. It is the only Serbia Maths book in hand — the expansion map's count and kind split (1 student, 0 teacher) are exact, and every record agrees. The file was read visually from full-page renders, every page, because its text layer is empty (a print-image PDF). It is the COMPLETE book: the CIP record gives 103 pages, the contents lists all seven units ending at printed p. 103, and the text runs printed pp. 1–103 followed by the imprint page (PDF p. 104). Printed-page basis: **printed page = PDF page** for the whole body, verified against printed folios throughout. Counting grain: one item per distinct taught skill or concept, at the point the book teaches it. The book's revision spreads (ОВО ЗНАМ / НАУЧИЛИ СМО) and its practice set (ЗАДАЦИ ЗА ВЕЖБАЊЕ) restate taught items and are named exclusions — except three places where a revision spread is the book's first presentation of new material (the number line and capacity-by-pouring on printed pp. 48–49, and balance-equivalence on printed p. 82), which are counted as teaching items and marked as such; in-lesson exercise sets are apparatus, not items. Also stocked nowhere: the front cover, contents, the seven unit divider pages and the imprint page, and the road-safety asides on printed p. 11 (no maths content). No book error was found in the book: its worked examples check out, and the contents page numbers agree with the printed folios throughout — a sheet-scale reading of printed p. 73 that seemed to omit 12 from the list of the numbers of the second ten was disproved on full-resolution re-check (the list is complete, 11, 12, 13, …, 20), an artefact of the reading scale, not a book error. Boundary, cited nowhere: Serbia's other 16 Maths files in the Kavčić free library (Grades 1–8 workbooks, textbooks and part-volumes, including the Grade 1 workbook *Математика 1 — радна свеска*) are link-only, not in hand and not read. One record-side note (reported, not fixed — merges never edit the records, and no maths/books/serbia.md filing exists or was created): textbooks.tsv secures only 6 of the 7 pushed Serbia books — the Grade 4 Serbian grammar book is in books.json and in the push state but has no TSV row; it is not a maths book and does not affect this reckoning. Into this lesson the placements stock 1 row — all from Book 1.

**Book 1:** *Математика 1* (Matematika 1 — Mathematics for the first grade of primary school) (pupil textbook) — Serbia, Нова школа (Nova škola), Belgrade, first edition 2018; authors Милица Ћук, Бранислав Милошевић, Бранко Марковић; ISBN 978-86-6225-041-4; ministry approval решение 650-02-00208/2018-07 of 27. 4. 2018; 135,143,758 bytes, 104 PDF pp., MD5 9a5df48784a319517cff29b5b407d0b2. Complete book: printed pp. 1–103 + covers and imprint; printed page = PDF page throughout. Read visually from full-page renders (the file's text layer is empty). PUPIL TEXTBOOK — Serbia is counted in this lesson's taught-in figure.

| Finding the unknown number (одређивање непознатог броја): the unknown written with a letter — an unknown addend (a + 5 = 8), an unknown minuend (a − 2 = 7) and an unknown subtrahend (10 − a = 4); any letter may stand for the unknown | Serbia — *Математика 1* (Grade 1 pupil textbook, Нова школа) — printed pp. 80–81 |

## Haiti added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Haiti's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **28 files, every one of them teacher-side**: the nine *Programme à Compétences Minimales* (PCM) volumes for Années Fondamentales 1–9 (MENFP, Direction de l'Enseignement Fondamental, November 2019), the three IPN *Programme détaillé, Matières de base* volumes (4e, 5e, 6e année), the three *Programme Pédagogique Opérationnel* (PPO) Mathématiques fascicules for the 3e cycle (7e, 8e, 9e année, 1989-1990), the eight Nectar (MENFP) teacher sequences (four for 7e année, four for 8e année), the Nouveau Secondaire *Programme-Cadre* and the four NS detailed programmes (NS1–NS4) — every one of the 28 carrying a full Mathématiques section — and placed **1,639 items, every one of them TEACHER/CURRICULUM EVIDENCE**: **Haiti has no pupil Maths textbook in hand under any label**, so Haiti is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure and is named in the countries line and in the rows below only, on the standing evidence-only precedent (Ghana / Bahamas / Canada / Myanmar). The expansion map's count for Haiti is wrong: it carries 15, counting only the books.json entries whose subject field reads “Mathematics”, and the download list claims 43 teacher files; the true maths-bearing set is 28 files — the nine “All subjects” PCM volumes, the three IPN detailed programmes and the NS Programme-Cadre all carry full maths sections the count missed (a record-side fault, reported, not fixed by this merge); textbooks.tsv is likewise a stale 28-row index naming only a subset of the secured files. Haiti's official pupil books (the MENFP/FAGEM *Manuels uniques* / “Mon manuel” series) are in hand for other subjects only, never for Mathematics, and the two remanié programme leads (a 7e année fascicule and a 4e année detailed programme) are not in hand — and programme documents are not textbooks under the 2026-11-10 textbook-test ruling in any case — so **Haiti stays PARTIAL for Maths, teacher evidence only**. All 28 files were read page-by-page from the secured PDFs in phase3-staging, byte-exact against their books.json records; nothing was downloaded. Read depth: the PPO 8e fascicule and the IPN 4e and 6e programmes are image-only scans and were read by OCR in full, spot-checked against the page images — where OCR garbled a worked example's digits, the placement teaches the corrected arithmetic and the case is flagged; PPO 7e PDF p. 48 and PPO 9e PDF pp. 20, 30, 31 and 32 were verified blank, separator or title pages carrying no objectives. Printed-page bases: PCM printed page = PDF page − 1; IPN 4e and 6e printed page = PDF page − 9, IPN 5e printed folios equal PDF pages; PPO 7e printed = PDF − 3, PPO 8e printed folio = PDF page, PPO 9e printed = PDF − 2; Programme-Cadre NS printed = PDF − 9 (verified against the detailed programme at §5.1–§5.2); NS1 and NS2 printed = PDF page, NS3 cites PDF pages, NS4 printed = PDF − 1; the Nectar sequences cite PDF pages by séance. The placements' eleven flags are all document-side and are carried here exactly: the PCM 6e volume's maths section header mislabels its level “5ème AF” (F1 — its items are cited to the 6e volume they stand in); PCM 5e's spoken-number row slips (“six mille deux” for 6,002, and a thousands-family slip in item 352's row) are placed corrected (F2); the PCM “orthocentre” definition error stands in the PCM **7e** volume — the diff's “(AF8)” label is the diff's own slip, disproven against the live PCM 8e section, which carries the correct circumcentre definition (F3); PCM 9e's right-asymptote and strict-inequality rows are completions of points the volume leaves silent, not errors (F4); PCM 9e's opening “années diagnostiques” ((6e), 7e, 8e) reinstate the diagnostic year the 3e-cycle fascicules dropped (F5); the PCM 7e volume announces a simple-interest objective that does not exist anywhere in its grids — nothing is placed for it, and simple interest is taught by PCM 9e and PPO 9e (F6); the Nectar “Relations entre les angles” sequence states a false corresponding-angles criterion and a mise en train with no solution, and the “Calcul littéral” sequence's séance 5 example drops a factor of x — all placed as corrected (F7, F8); the record-side faults (the map's count, the download list's count, the stale textbooks.tsv) are reported, not fixed (F9); the IPN 6e worked examples carry numerical inconsistencies (objectives 5.1, 7.1, 12.2, 16.1, 19.1, 21.1, 22.1) flagged as read and NOT asserted as print errors — the volume is an OCR-read scan and some may be digit misreads (F10); and NS2's own dating (2015 in the detailed programmes' shared headers, the school year 2008-2009 on its cover) is the book's own, not a discrepancy (F11). The placements' named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the PCM volumes' other-discipline sections and their week-by-week scheduling columns beyond each distinct objective; the IPN volumes' maths avant-propos, theme indexes, other-subject sections and Suggestions/Évaluation apparatus (except where an activity is itself the taught content); the PPO fascicules' shared front matter, Programme-Cadre theme tables, progression grids, bibliographies and annexes; the Nectar sequences' summative-evaluation séances and session-management apparatus (except where a séance's activity is the taught content); the Programme-Cadre's other subjects, and the Nouveau Secondaire framework chapters shared between it and the NS1/NS2 volumes (placed once, from the Programme-Cadre volume); and the verified blank and separator pages. Into this lesson the placements stock 17 items — all teacher/curriculum-evidence rows — from Book 1 (*PCM, 7e AF (MENFP, 2019)*), Book 2 (*PCM, 8e AF (MENFP, 2019)*), Book 3 (*PCM, 9e AF (MENFP, 2019)*), Book 4 (*PPO Mathématiques, 7e année (1989-1990)*), Book 5 (*PPO Mathématiques, 8e année (1989-1990)*), Book 6 (*PPO Mathématiques, 9e année (1989-1990)*), Book 7 (*Nectar «Calcul littéral» (8e année)*), Book 8 (*Programme-Cadre NS (MENFP)*) and Book 9 (*NS1 Programme détaillé (MENFP)*).

## Suriname added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Suriname's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **18 books: 13 pupil Maths books, 3 teacher Maths books and 2 kindergarten curriculum books** (20 files examined; two further in-hand files, the LJ3 OJW *Leerlingenwerkboek* and *Ik-boek*, were checked and excluded — drawing and orientation activities with no rekenen strand and no number teaching) — and placed **376 items: 344 pupil teaching items, 7 teacher-evidence items and 25 curriculum-evidence items**. No Suriname row existed in any maths lesson before this merge, so every placed item is new here. **Set verdict, proved from the books themselves:** the expansion map's count is right for the Mathematics-labelled set (13 pupil + 3 teacher = 16 files), but its kind labels are wrong in three places: (1) the LJ6 “Leerkrachtenhandleiding Deel 1” of the records is the programme's **Algemene Inleiding** volume (Book 14), not a lesson-by-lesson guide to Deel 1; (2) the two LJ7 teacher books are **oplossingenboeken — answer keys** (Books 15–16), teacher evidence; (3) Book 4 is a **rekenboek** (lesson book), not the “Leerlingenboek” textbooks.tsv names it — the same book. **Maths was hiding under another subject label once:** the KG *Spelend leren* Themaboek 3 and 4 (Books 17–18), filed as “Play-based learning” with textbooks.tsv kind “student” — also wrong: they are **teacher curriculum books** — each carry twelve *voorbereidend rekenen* (preparatory maths) sections with stated learning goals; their 24 items are stocked as **curriculum evidence**, never pupil teaching. **Partial volumes, named as such:** Books 4 and 5 are **Deel A only** and Book 12 is **Deel 1 only**; the download list's remark that LJ8 Deel 2 is “already held” is **not borne out by any record in hand** (textbooks.tsv, the staging books.json and the push-state carry only Deel 1 for LJ8) — LJ8 Deel 2 is treated as not in hand, and the LJ4/LJ5 B-volumes and LJ8 Deel 2–3 are link-only or unrecorded, cited nowhere. Book 8 (LJ6 Deel 3) is staged and pushed but has no textbooks.tsv row — the map counted it, the TSV does not carry it. **The six flagged claims are carried exactly:** (F1) **Book 11 (LJ7 Deel 3) has no Les 24** — contents and body both jump from Les 23 (tangram) to Les 25 (tekenen op schaal); the book holds 38 lessons, not 39, and the rows cite the 38 that exist; (F2) the LJ8-Deel-2 “already held” claim, above — contradicted, not in hand; (F3) **book error, Book 6 Les 6 (printed p. 23):** the information block adds a quarter hour to 15.45 and gives the result as “06:00” — the correct result is **16:00**, and the row teaches the corrected form; (F4) **book error, Book 7 Les 14 (printed p. 54):** the information states the area of a rectangle with a garbled formula line (the perimeter formula 2l + 2b is given correctly, and the area is worked by counting in the same lesson) — the row teaches perimeter = 2l + 2b and area by counting/calculation, the forms the lesson's own worked examples use; (F5) **book error, Book 6 Les 30 (printed p. 93):** the split-subtraction check line prints minus signs where the method needs the parts added (7,986 − 5,234 split as 2,000 + 700 + 50 + 2 = 2,752) — the method itself is taught correctly in Les 21 and the rows follow the correct form; (F6) **Book 13's kind:** textbooks.tsv files the LJ10 Reader as a pupil book; its foreword states it is a guide “voor de reken-leerkracht en leerling” (for teacher and pupil) — its items are placed as teaching content, worded as a **programme reader**, not a pupil textbook. **Text-layer caveat (not a claim):** Book 3's text layer drops the digit “1” in several headings (“splitsingen van 0” for the splitting of 10); the rows use the true values, read from the sums and context. **Checked and NOT flagged:** the answer keys (Books 15–16) match their pupil books lesson for lesson (39 and 38 lessons respectively), and Book 14's contents listing of the kwartaal-1 lessons matches Book 6. Byte sizes checkable against the gov.sr records matched exactly (Books 7, 8, 10, 11). Printed-page basis: printed page = PDF page in every book except Books 17–18 (printed = PDF − 5). The placements' named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the *Vooruit* books' week-closing revision pages (*weekafsluitingen*); Books 1–3's *herhaling* revision worksheets, *oefentoets* practice tests, *knipbladen* cut-out sheets and automatisation games, except where a sheet first teaches a point (those are placed); the *Plezier* books' closing games; Book 13's ch. 1 programme tables beyond item R1; and Books 17–18's non-maths theme sections. The *Plezier in rekenen* companion volumes found by the 2026-11-10 gov.sr census (LJ4 B leerlingenboek, LJ4 werkboek/takenboek, LJ5 takenboek/bijwerkboek) are link-only finds, not in hand — not read, not counted. Suriname counts in a lesson's taught-in figure only on the strength of its pupil rows; its teacher rows and curriculum rows are evidence — cited as the evidence kind they are and never the basis of the count (the standing Ghana evidence precedent). Into this lesson the placements stock 1 items (1 pupil) from Book 8 (*Vooruit met Rekenen, Deel 3 (LJ6)*).

**Book 8:** *Vooruit met Rekenen — Deel 3, leerlingenboek, Leerjaar 6* (pupil textbook) — Suriname, MinOWC, 2023; 2,709,444 bytes, 136 PDF pp. Pupil textbook. Printed page = PDF page (byte size matches the gov.sr record exactly). Kwartaal 3, lessons 1–39. (The book is staged and pushed but carries no textbooks.tsv row — a record gap, reported, not fixed by this merge.)

## Papua New Guinea added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Papua New Guinea’s whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **32 files = 28 works, all Papua New Guinea Department of Education (NDoE), “Issued free to schools by the Department of Education”: 8 pupil textbook files (7 works — the JICA/QUIS-ME National Mathematics Textbooks, Grades 1–6, with Grades 3 and 5 in two parts each) and 24 teacher files (21 works — 9 JICA teacher’s manual files, 6 works with Grades 3–5 in two parts each, and 15 Standards Based Curriculum (SBC) teacher guides, Elementary Prep to Grade 12, with separate Advanced and General guides at Grades 11 and 12)** — and placed 503 items (102 from the pupil textbooks, 401 teacher-evidence items from the manuals and guides). The expansion map is wrong on both of its numbers (flag 1 — record-side, reported, not fixed by this merge): it said 6 student + 22 teacher in hand; the true set is 8 student + 24 teacher. The map missed three files that exist only as textbooks.tsv rows (the Grade 4 and Grade 6 full-year JICA pupil textbooks and the Grade 6 JICA manual) and did not know the Grade 2 SBC guide, which has a staging books.json row but no textbooks.tsv row and no push-state entry (flag 4). **Filing error (flag 2 — record-side): the git-tree copies recorded for the Grade 1 and Grade 2 JICA manuals are a different country’s book** — both TSV tree paths hold the identical file, a Mozambique Grade 1 teacher manual in Portuguese (“Manual do Professor, 1.ª classe”, 5,891,019 B / 216 pp, the same MD5 at both paths — the only byte-identical duplicate pair found in this hunt, and not a PNG book), which also sits in the release `mid-size-books-8` correctly named as a Mozambique book. The genuine PNG manuals were read from the GitHub release `full-quality-oversized-books`, whose assets match the staged sizes and checklist MD5s exactly; the push-state’s per-folder landing claims for those two files should be treated as unreliable until the tree paths are re-filed. Flag 3: the two manuals’ recorded education.gov.pg source URLs do not serve the staged pressings — both returned an HTML page during the read, and a later re-probe served a different pressing of the Grade 1 manual — so the staged pressings cannot be re-grabbed from the recorded source URLs. Flag 5: the Grade 9 SBC guide’s textbooks.tsv tree path 404s; the file was recovered by searching all release assets and matches books.json on size and pages. No Maths hides under other subject labels in the PNG records — all 156 staging books.json entries and all 163 textbooks.tsv rows were checked by subject, title and path. Link-only Maths not in hand and not read (it does not count against closure): the FODE distance course-books for Grades 7–12 Mathematics / Maths A / Maths G (unit PDFs) and the two OBE upper-secondary guides found 2026-11-10 (Advanced Mathematics TG, ISBN 978-9980-9924-8-2; General Mathematics TG). Book errors found on the read are taught corrected: the Grade 1 Textbook and its manual print “Subtracation” in the Chapter/Unit 12 title; the SBC guides print “Angels” for Angles in places, “Algebrain” for Algebraic (a Grade 11 guide’s Factorisation unit header) and “Orobability” for Probability (the Grade 9 guide’s strand table); the Grade 1 SBC guide’s Term-1 record prints “tenths” where tens is meant. The Grade 3 SBC programme has no Money topic where the JICA Grade 3 textbook carries one — a programme difference, not an error. Printed-page basis: JICA textbook pages cite the book’s own printed folio / contents page; JICA manuals cite the manual’s own printed contents page; SBC guides cite the guide’s printed programme/contents pages (the basis is stated in each book entry below); where a contents extraction was partly two-column-interleaved, the PDF page is given as well and marked “PDF p.”. Counting grain: one item per chapter (pupil textbooks), per unit (teacher’s manuals — the manual unit mirrors the textbook chapter of the same number and is counted once, as teacher evidence), and per named topic/unit in the SBC guides’ own programmes. The placements’ named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the end-of-book/year “Summary” chapters and units in the JICA textbooks and manuals (review apparatus), and the SBC guides’ front sections, assessment-record templates, glossaries/resources and integrated cross-subject project sections. Pupil-textbook rows are COUNTED normally; every manual and SBC guide row is teacher evidence — named in the countries line and in the rows, but outside the acceptance count. Into this lesson the placements stock 4 items — 0 pupil-textbook rows and 4 teacher-evidence rows — from Book T11 (*Mathematics Teacher Guide, Elementary One (SBC)*), Book T13 (*Mathematics Teacher Guide Grade 3 (SBC)*), Book T14 (*Mathematics Teacher Guide Grade 4 (SBC)*), Book T17 (*Mathematics Teacher Guide Grade 7 (SBC)*).
