## Sweden added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Sweden's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **13 files, 594 PDF pages**, of which **10 carry mathematics**: the national curriculum Lgr22, 2 SPSM pupil materials, and 7 SPSM teacher guides, notes and support publications. Sweden has **no state pupil textbook series** — textbook approval was abolished in 1991, Skolverket sets the curriculum (Lgr22), and the state producer SPSM (Specialpedagogiska skolmyndigheten) publishes special-needs materials — so the pupil teaching in hand is SPSM material for blind and deaf pupils, and nothing else: 13 pupil items (the Abakus exercise book and the Växelspelet money game), 81 teacher-evidence items and 79 curriculum-evidence items from Lgr22's Matematik kursplan, 173 items in all. The other 3 files in the read set prove on reading to carry no maths at all and are stocked nowhere: *Skrivmaterial för elever som behöver förstärkta linjer och rutor* is blank ruled/grid stationery (copying masters), the *ABC Skriva-Läsa* teacher guide is literacy only, and a generically named *Arbetsblad* worksheet (pharmacy vocabulary) was pulled in as a hidden-maths spot check and cleared negative. The expansion map is wrong on both axes (flag F4): it listed 8 files as 3 student + 5 teacher, but two of its eight are not Maths at all (F1: the Skriva-Läsa guide, filed in books.json under “Matematik; Svenska”, is a literacy guide — its only number-adjacent moment, drawing a numbered slip 2–5 to pick that many pictures (printed p. 6), is classroom apparatus; F3: the Skrivmaterial, counted as a student Maths book, is blank stationery), a third is the wrong kind (F2: *Räkna med mig*, filed as a student book, is a 105-page handbook for teachers of braille-using pupils — all 35 of its items are teacher evidence), and four maths-bearing files were missed by the map entirely — Lgr22 itself (its section 5.5 is the full Matematik syllabus), the *Arbeta med språkstörning i matematikundervisningen* support publication (filed under generic special education), *Växelspelet* (filed under “Verklighetsuppfattning”) and the *Jag handlar* teacher guide (filed under home-economics subjects). Flags F1–F4 are record/map-side: they are reported here and carried in this section; the records themselves are not edited — merges never edit the records, and no maths/books/sweden.md filing exists. The books' own errors are carried corrected: F5 — both Abakus volumes misprint their print ISBNs with a “987-” prefix (Övningsbok “987-91-28-11602-2”, Handledning “987-91-28-11600-8”; each book's PDF ISBN carries the correct 978- prefix); F6 — *Räkna med mig*'s ISBN is printed malformed as “98-91-28-10386-2”; F7 — the Abakus Handledning's division chapter (printed p. 45) says the pupil must master “multiplikation” where the sense requires division, taught corrected on the division row it touches; F8 — a box in *Räkna med mig* (printed p. 38) misnames the Abakus handbook as “Räkna med mig – handledning”. The placements' record items are stocked nowhere: R1 (Lgr22's assessment criteria, printed pp. 60–63 — they restate the central content as standards and add no content beyond the items), R2 (the Abakus Handledning's exercise sets and maths-card copying masters), R3 (the Arbeta publication's overview material), R4 (*Räkna med mig*'s apparatus), R5 (the Diamanterna card game itself is a physical product, not a book, and is not in hand — only its one-page SPSM note is) and R6 (the *Jag handlar* pupil book is recorded only as a sample that was never staged as a secured file; only its teacher guide is in hand). One basis note: the Övningsbok's multiplication and division exercise pages are abacus diagrams with no printed numerals, so those two placements rest on the book's contents page, section headings and introduction, exactly as the placements state. Sweden's class for Maths is **PARTIAL (evidence-heavy)**: every in-hand Maths-bearing file has been read and stocked, but 160 of its 173 items are teacher or curriculum evidence and its pupil side is special-needs material only. Into this lesson the placements stock 9 items: 5 curriculum evidence, 4 teacher evidence. No Sweden pupil item stocks into this lesson, so Sweden is added to the countries line above and named in the rows below as evidence only, and is NOT added to the acceptance count, on the standing evidence-only precedent.

**Book 1:** *Läroplan för grundskolan, förskoleklassen och fritidshemmet 2022* (Lgr22) (national curriculum) — Sweden, Skolverket, 2024; 1,317,913 bytes, 272 PDF pp., MD5 ea52068ef1b63a71f80885ddc7abdf95. Read for its Matematik kursplan in full (section 5.5, printed pp. 54–63) plus the förskoleklass chapter's maths content; the book's other 18 subject syllabi are out of scope for a Maths read. CURRICULUM EVIDENCE ONLY — Lgr22 states what must be taught and teaches nothing itself; its rows are worded as the requirements they are and are counted in no lesson's taught-in figure. Its assessment criteria (printed pp. 60–63) restate the central content as standards and are record item R1, stocked nowhere.

**Book 9:** *Arbeta med språkstörning i matematikundervisningen* (support publication) — Sweden, SPSM, 2021, ISBN 978-91-28-00992-8; 764,290 bytes, 26 PDF pp., MD5 60b56c2eb928a6983dceccca12e03797. SPSM support publication on the language of mathematics for pupils with developmental language disorder. It teaches no pupil maths directly; the items placed from it are the concrete maths content it names, demonstrates or uses as worked material, placed as teacher evidence. Its overview material — the catalogue of known difficulty points (printed p. 4), its concrete-versus-abstract discussion (p. 9) and its general support catalogue (pp. 15, 17) — is record item R3, stocked nowhere. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY.

**Book 10:** *Räkna med mig* (teacher handbook) — Sweden, SPSM, 2018, best.nr 10386; 20,465,920 bytes, 105 PDF pp., MD5 5fbeac506f239001f466a59aa7574d9e. A 105-page handbook on mathematics teaching for compulsory-school pupils who use braille, addressed to teachers (foreword, printed p. 7) and built on the Lgr11 central content; grabbed from its recorded source_url after the GitHub Contents API returned 404. Filed in the records as a student book — wrong (flag F2): it is not a pupil book, and all 35 of its items are teacher evidence. Book errors: its ISBN is printed malformed as “98-91-28-10386-2” (flag F6), and a box on printed p. 38 misnames the Abakus handbook as “Räkna med mig – handledning” (flag F8); the Abakus guide placed from Book 4 is the real book. Its apparatus — the accessible-learning-environment and adapted/national tests chapters (printed pp. 14–26), the tools and materials catalogue (pp. 85–91), the maths word list (p. 95), the table of common signs in maths braille (pp. 96–97) and the copying masters (pp. 98–103) — is record item R4, stocked nowhere. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY.

## Switzerland added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Switzerland's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **8 files / 6 works, and no pupil textbook among them**: the three PER brochures *Mathématiques et Sciences de la nature / Sciences humaines et sociales*, Cycles 1, 2 and 3 (CIIP, Version 3.0, août 2024), whose Mathématiques parts are placed here; the Ticino *Piano di studio della scuola dell'obbligo ticinese* (DECS, edizione perfezionata 2022), whose Matematica chapter is placed here; the *Lehrplan 21 Graubünden* (Fassung vom 15.03.2016), whose Mathematik plan is placed here — one work in three filed language editions (German, Romansh, Italian), read in full in the German edition, verified identical in structure and pagination in the other two, and cited once; and one teacher guide, *MaMa — Linee guida del progetto MaMa: Ambito Numeri e calcolo* (DECS/CeRDD, 2021), whose didactic rulings are placed here as teacher evidence — 425 items in all (364 curriculum evidence + 61 teacher-guide evidence, 0 pupil). The expansion map's kind split is WRONG (record-level; the records themselves are not edited — merges never edit the records): it recorded "3 student + 1 teacher", but the three "student" files are the PER **curriculum** brochures mislabelled — **Switzerland has 0 pupil Maths textbooks in hand**, and its prescribed pupil series (Schweizer Zahlenbuch, Mathematik Primarstufe / Sekundarstufe I (LMVZ), Mathwelt, Mathbuch, MER Mathématiques 1–11, Ticino's Base Matematica 1–4) are commercial and licence-gated, not in hand and not read. Further maths was found hiding under "all subjects" labels: the Ticino Piano and the Lehrplan 21 Graubünden are all-subjects volumes carrying full mathematics chapters. Every grabbed file was verified SHA-256 byte-exact against its books.json record. Checked in hand and excluded with reasons, stocked nowhere: the three PER *Aperçus des contenus* (one example-grain maths page each — record items R1–R3), the PER *Présentation générale* (framework structure only), the PER *Éducation numérique* brochure (the computer-science curriculum; mathematics appears only as cross-links — a separate school subject) and the four Vaud *<DÉ>CODAGE* digital-education teacher manuals (contents pages of all four read: Science informatique / Médias / Usages only — a separate school subject); inside the PER brochures, the Sciences de la nature and Sciences humaines et sociales parts are other subjects, the *Modélisation* objectives are process apparatus whose content is carried by the per-theme problem-solving items, and the Lexiques are apparatus; inside the Ticino volume, every chapter other than Matematica is another subject and the process/attitude competence targets carry no subject content; inside the Lehrplan 21 volumes, every subject other than Mathematik is out of scope and the Einführung/Gesamtüberblick chapters are structure, not content; MaMa §§1 and 3 are project apparatus, and §2's quoted Ticino targets are placed at the Ticino items, not double-counted (record item R4). One within-document inconsistency is flagged so it is not stocked: the PER Cycle 1 *Aperçus des contenus* claims Cycle 1 pupils "calculate lengths (perimeter of a rectangle), areas, volumes (cube)" — the governing Cycle 1 brochure requires only comparison and measurement with an étalon (items 46–55), and perimeter/area/volume calculation begins in Cycle 2 (items 158–160); the brochure governs, the Aperçu's Cycle 1 sentence is an overstatement, and it is NOT stocked as a Cycle 1 requirement (record item R1). One document error is called out by a source itself and taught corrected: the MaMa guide rules that there is no "dissociative property" of addition — splitting an addend and associating (16 + 19 = 16 + 10 + 9) is the associative property, and textbooks that name it otherwise are wrong (item 378); the guide's other flagged classroom errors are misconceptions it warns against, not errors it commits. No Liechtenstein file is judged here: Liechtenstein teaches from Switzerland's books, but no file is filed under Liechtenstein in any record. Printed-page basis: PER brochures, Ticino Piano and Lehrplan 21 — printed folio = PDF page − 2; MaMa — printed page = PDF page. Switzerland's class for Maths is **PARTIAL (evidence-only)**: every in-hand Maths-bearing file has been read and stocked, but all 425 of its items are curriculum or teacher-guide evidence and no pupil textbook is in hand. Into this lesson the placements stock 4 items (4 curriculum evidence) from Book 3 and Book 6. Switzerland is added to the countries line and named in the rows below as evidence only, and is NOT added to the acceptance count, on the standing evidence-only precedent.

**Book 3:** *Mathématiques et Sciences de la nature / Sciences humaines et sociales — Cycle 3* (curriculum brochure) — Switzerland, CIIP (Conférence intercantonale de l'instruction publique de la Suisse romande et du Tessin), Plan d'études romand (PER), Version 3.0, août 2024; 1,241,996 bytes, 116 PDF pp.; SHA-256 verified exact against its books.json record. Printed folio = PDF p. − 2. Only the Mathématiques part (printed pp. 14–35) is placed here; the Sciences de la nature and Sciences humaines et sociales parts of the same brochure are other subjects. Filed in the records as a student book — wrong: it is a curriculum brochure. CURRICULUM EVIDENCE ONLY — the brochure states what must be taught and teaches nothing itself; its rows are worded as the requirements they are and are counted in no lesson's taught-in figure.

**Book 6:** *Lehrplan 21 Graubünden — Gesamtausgabe* (cantonal curriculum) — Switzerland, Erziehungs-, Kultur- und Umweltschutzdepartement Graubünden, Fassung vom 15.03.2016; German edition 60,184,274 bytes, 494 PDF pp.; SHA-256 verified exact against its books.json record. Printed folio = PDF p. − 2. ONE work in three filed language editions — German (read in full), Romansh *Plan d'instrucziun 21* (57,219,009 bytes, 488 PDF pp.) and Italian *Piano di studio 21 Grigioni* (56,644,563 bytes, 492 PDF pp.), both verified as the same work in translation (identical structure, pagination and MA competency codes) — and cited once. Only the Mathematik chapter (printed pp. 194–221) is placed here; every other subject in the volume is out of scope. CURRICULUM EVIDENCE ONLY — the plan states the competencies pupils must reach and teaches nothing itself; its rows are counted in no lesson's taught-in figure.

## Thailand added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Thailand's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **6 pupil textbooks: the IPST (Institute for the Promotion of Teaching Science and Technology, Ministry of Education) *Additional Mathematics* series, Matthayom 4–6, Books 1–2** — and placed 267 items. The expansion map's count and kind split are right: staging `books.json` holds exactly these 6 entries (all `subject: Mathematics`, all `kind: student`), `textbooks.tsv` carries exactly these 6 Thailand rows across all subjects — so no Maths book hides under another subject label — and `phase3-push-state.json` lists 5 of the 6 as pushed. Each book was re-fetched for the read — from the repo tree (Contents API) or the named release where the pusher had stored it, else from the book's recorded `source_url` (Internet Archive) — verified by byte size against its record, and the grabbed copies were deleted after their placements were written. Printed page = PDF page − 12 in all six books: the front matter runs cover → title → preface → course description → contents (contents on PDF pp. 10–11, PDF p. 12 blank) and the body opens at printed p. 1 on PDF p. 13, verified against printed folios at chapter starts in every book. Counting grain: one item per distinct teaching point — a named concept, method, rule or worked skill with its printed page. The placements' named exclusions — exercise sets and end-of-chapter test sets in all six books; worked-example repeats; chapter openers and historical sidebars; front matter; and back matter (bibliography, the bilingual Thai–English glossary appendix and production credits) — are stocked nowhere. Book errors found in the read are taught corrected, the corrected form being what the items teach. The read's one flagged claim rides with the set, honestly (flag F1): the `textbooks.tsv` GitHub blob URL for M5 Book 1 is dead — the authenticated Contents API lookup returns 404, the file is absent from `phase3-push-state.json` and the staging copy is gone — but the book itself is fully confirmed, re-fetched from its recorded `source_url` (Internet Archive `math3_text.pdf`) at 6,204,831 bytes, matching `books.json` exactly, so the flag is on the GitHub filing claim only, not on the book, and the library's pushed count of 5 for Thailand is consistent with it. The two pending library rulings do not bite on this set: all six books are official IPST textbooks, so no approved-publisher question arises, and no in-hand file is a flipbook capture or publisher sample — every file is the complete book; the elibrarycub “Sample_ebook” files and AnyFlip candidates in the download list are link-only, not in hand and not read. Thailand COUNTS in this lesson's taught-in figure: every Thailand book is a pupil textbook. Into this lesson the placements stock 1 item, every one a pupil-textbook row, from Book 1 (*Additional Mathematics, Matthayom 4, Book 1*).

**Book 1:** *Additional Mathematics, Matthayom 4, Book 1* — IPST (Institute for the Promotion of Teaching Science and Technology), Ministry of Education; e-book 1st printing B.E. 2563. Held in the repo tree (Contents API blob); byte size matches the Internet Archive record for the same scan exactly. The filed copy is a scan with no text layer — read through the Internet Archive's own OCR text derivative of the identical scan (item `math1_202412`), with the cover, title page and contents sighted directly in the filed scan itself. 17,299,823 bytes, 232 PDF pp. Printed page = PDF page − 12.

## Uganda added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Uganda's whole in-hand Maths library from the PDFs — **8 files / 8 works: 7 pupil books + 1 teacher's guide**, all Uganda Ministry of Education and Sports / NCDC *Home-Study Learning* books (August 2020) — and the expansion map's “7 student + 1 teacher, FILES-IN-HAND” count and split are **correct**, with one correction of kind: the Primary Five pupil book is not a standalone Maths book — it is the Mathematics booklet (package PDF pp. 9–166) inside the NCDC Primary Five merged home-study package (Mathematics + Integrated Science + English + Social Studies, 350 pp.). Maths content hiding under an all-subjects label was checked for across the whole in-hand set; this package is the only such volume. The seven pupil books (Primary Four, the Primary Five booklet, Primary Six, Senior One, Senior Two, Senior Three, Senior Four) are real pupil teaching books — lessons, worked examples and exercises addressed to the learner — so Uganda's pupil rows **count** in a lesson's taught-in figure. The one teacher book, the Senior One *Teacher's Guide* (prototype, 76 pp.), is **teacher evidence only** and counts nowhere; it covers all 12 Senior One prototype topics, including five topics (1 Number Bases, 2 Working with Integers, 8 Angle Properties, 9 Data Collection and Presentation, 12 Time and Time Tables) for which no Senior One pupil text is in hand, and its evidence for those topics carries that “no pupil book in hand” caveat. NOT in hand, and cited nowhere: the Senior One prototype pupil Mathematics textbook (NCDC posted only the Teacher's Guide) and the Scribd combined S1&S2 Science Package (no download route). The placements number **324 items: 278 pupil items and 46 teacher-evidence items (TG1–TG46)** — one item per distinct lesson in the home-study books, one per distinct topic treatment in the guide. Page citations are PDF pages for the pupil books (in the primary home-study books the printed folios run 6 behind: printed = PDF − 6; Primary Five pages are the merged package's PDF pages) and the guide's printed pages for the TG items. The books' own errors are taught corrected: the Senior One home-study book heads the conversion example at PDF p. 8 “Convert 13/4 into an improper fraction” — 13/4 is already improper, and the worked example in fact converts it to the mixed number 3¼; the Primary Six book's triangle-angles exercise (PDF p. 85) prints two interior angles as “360 and 540” — the degree signs set as zeros, read as 36° and 54°; the Senior Three book heads its mensuration topic “TRIGONOMETRY” while its own stated outcomes and lessons are units, surface area and volume — taught as mensuration. Uganda's own ways are kept: equations written with a shape for the unknown before letters (Primary Four); integers walked on a ground number line, face forward for positive and back for backward (Primary Five and Six); base five taught in primary school (Primary Five); fractions taught by slicing chapattis (Senior One); income tax worked from Uganda's own tax bands and allowances (Senior Three). Uganda's class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED** — every Maths book in hand has been read fresh and placed; what remains outside the set (the Senior One prototype pupil textbook, the Senior Two–Four prototype books and guides) is not in hand and is a library hunting matter, not a course hold. Into this lesson the placements stock 6 pupil items.

**Book 1:** *Mathematics Home-Study Learning, Primary Four* (pupil book) — Uganda, Ministry of Education and Sports / National Curriculum Development Centre (NCDC), August 2020; 3,968,012 bytes, 74 PDF pp., MD5 72ec063ca3c0ecb939a2c5e9a63a2412. Cover (“HOME-STUDY LEARNING / PRIMARY 4 / MATHEMATICS / August 2020”) and NCDC colophon read directly. Pages are cited by PDF page; the printed folios run 6 behind (printed = PDF − 6). PUPIL BOOK — its rows count in a lesson's taught-in figure.

**Book 2:** *Home-Study Learning, Primary Five* — the Mathematics booklet only (pupil book) — Uganda, Ministry of Education and Sports / NCDC, August 2020; the Primary Five book is not a standalone Maths book: it is the Mathematics booklet (package PDF pp. 9–166, 158 pp.) inside the merged home-study package (Mathematics + Integrated Science + English + Social Studies); the package file is 10,444,968 bytes, 350 PDF pp., MD5 5159920c66cfd1869ff19fd9cf119fea, and the booklet's interior is verified as the Primary Five Mathematics home-study book. Pages are cited by the package's PDF page. PUPIL BOOK — its rows count in a lesson's taught-in figure.

**Book 3:** *Mathematics Home-Study Learning, Primary Six* (pupil book) — Uganda, Ministry of Education and Sports / NCDC, August 2020; 1,758,562 bytes, 98 PDF pp., MD5 2046f27f8e3df0cc1c9b274f5b17a776. ## Samoa added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Samoa's whole in-hand Maths library from the PDFs — **9 files: 8 pupil textbooks + 1 curriculum statement, 0 teacher guides** — and the expansion map's “7 student Maths books, 0 teacher” count is **wrong**: the map missed the *Mathematics Year 12* typeset edition, staged in the records as a supposed workbook (“full-year-wk”) but proved from its title page and interior to be the complete MESC Year 12 pupil textbook (Units 1–8 + answers), and it missed the kind of the *Primary Curriculum Statement, Years 1–8*, which is a curriculum document, not a pupil book. The library's enumerated slot for the separate 113 MB scanned Year 12 edition is NOT in hand (live 404; only a truncated 1 MB archive stub exists) — that edition is cited nowhere and the slot stays a library-side matter; the complete typeset Year 12 pupil textbook in hand is the book read. No teacher guide for Maths exists in the Samoa set, and no Maths content was found hiding under another subject label. The eight pupil books are real pupil teaching books — lessons, worked examples and exercises addressed to the learner — so Samoa's pupil rows **count** in a lesson's taught-in figure. The curriculum statement (CMAD, MESC, January 2013) states what is REQUIRED to be taught in Samoa primary schools and teaches nothing itself: its rows are **curriculum evidence only** and count in no taught-in figure — Samoa has no primary pupil textbooks in hand, so every primary (Years 1–8) Samoa row in the course is curriculum evidence. Counting grain: one item per distinct teaching section of a pupil book (the book's own numbered sections and their named subsections), and one item per coded learning outcome (NR / PA / DA / MS / SG / WM codes) of the curriculum statement; worked examples and exercise sets inside a section are part of that section's item, not separate items. The books' own errors are taught corrected: Year 10 Book 2's §7.1 heading prints “Drawing an Englargement” (for Enlargement); Year 11 Book 2's unit opener prints “Dealing with Angles Larger than 900” (for 90°); Year 13's contents lists chapter 2 as “Linear Inequations” while the chapter's printed title is “Linear Equations” and it teaches both — placed under the printed title. Named exclusions, stocked nowhere: the answers sections of every pupil book; Year 13's “Problems and Investigations” end-of-chapter sections, its Glossary/Index and its formula Appendix; calculator-keystroke asides (the mathematics is placed, the keystrokes are not); the curriculum statement's strand progression tables (printed pp. 81–120), which restate its coded outcomes; and Year 12's Unit 8 project unit, placed as one item. Into this lesson the placements stock 14 items: 14 pupil.

**Book 1:** *Mathematics Year 9 Book One* (pupil book) — Samoa, Ministry of Education, Sports and Culture (MESC); © 2001, reprinted 2004; ISBN 982-517-010-7; 1,483,605 bytes, 188 PDF pp., MD5 b7876195d3f78ec9ca557ff5dbc29521. Cover and title page read directly. Printed page = PDF page − 1. PUPIL BOOK — its rows count in a lesson's taught-in figure.

## United States added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read the United States' whole in-hand Maths library from the PDFs — **5 pupil textbooks, 0 teacher guides, all OpenStax** (the CC BY-NC-SA 4.0 open-textbook line of Rice University, Houston, Texas) — and the expansion map's “5 student Maths books, 0 teacher” count is **right**. The United States has no national textbook series and no federal textbook: the set is one nonprofit publisher's open-textbook line, adopted course by course across US states and districts, and every book is a genuine pupil textbook — exposition, worked examples and practice addressed to the student — so the United States' rows **count** in a lesson's taught-in figure. Three record-level flags ride with the set; none of them is a course claim. **F1 — the “Grade 13” filing hides the level split:** four of the five books are college textbooks filed at “Grade 13” (*Introductory Statistics 2e*, *Calculus Volume 1*, *College Algebra 2e*, *Algebra and Trigonometry 2e*); only *Algebra 1* is a school book (the US high-school Algebra 1 course, filed Grade 12), and the set is never presented as five school-grade books. **F2 — the push-state is silent but GitHub holds the files:** `phase3-push-state.json` has no United States entry and nothing US sits in phase3-staging, yet all five files were verified live on GitHub at their exact recorded byte sizes and were read from there — the books ARE in hand and no re-push is owed. **F3 — two of the books duplicate each other:** *Algebra and Trigonometry 2e* repeats *College Algebra 2e* section for section in 59 of its 79 sections (its Chapters 1–6 keep the same pagination), so the country's distinct teaching is 299 items of the 358 placed; both books are nevertheless cited for their own in-hand copies. Honest limit, stated plainly: the in-hand coverage is top-end only — one high-school book and four college books; no US elementary or middle-school Maths book is in hand (the Texas Bluebonnet Learning sets and the New York EngageNY/Eureka Math modules in the library records are link-only, and the US set's OpenStax *Principles of Microeconomics for AP Courses 2e* was checked and excluded — an economics textbook, not mathematics). Counting grain: one item per numbered teaching section of a book (*Algebra 1*: one item per numbered lesson; a lesson's sub-activities are its steps, not separate items). Named exclusions, stocked nowhere: chapter introductions, chapter reviews, formula reviews, practice and homework sets, appendices, answer keys and calculator notes in every book; *Introductory Statistics 2e*'s 22 guided lab and experiment sections (record items R1–R22 — a chapter's teaching applied to one data set, not counted); *Algebra 1*'s unit overviews, readiness reviews and wrap-ups and its nine unit projects. Into this lesson the placements stock 13 items — 7 from *Algebra 1*, 3 from *College Algebra 2e* and 3 from *Algebra and Trigonometry 2e*.

**Book 1:** *Algebra 1* (pupil book) — OpenStax, Rice University, Houston, Texas; senior contributing authors Lynn Marecek, MaryAnne Anthony-Smith, Andrea Honeycutt Mathis, Jay Abramson, Sharon North, Amy Baldwin and Alyssa Howell; © 2025 Rice University, CC BY-NC-SA 4.0; 82,715,252 bytes, 1,535 PDF pp. OpenStax's high-school Algebra 1 pupil course book, filed Grade 12 — the one true school book of the United States set. Printed page = PDF page − 34 in the body. PUPIL BOOK — its rows count in a lesson's taught-in figure.

**Book 2:** *College Algebra 2e* (pupil book) — OpenStax, Rice University, Houston, Texas; senior contributing author Jay Abramson (Arizona State University); © 2026 Rice University, CC BY-NC-SA 4.0; 84,220,165 bytes, 1,073 PDF pp. A college pupil textbook, filed at “Grade 13” in the library. Printed pages are the book's own folios. PUPIL BOOK — its rows count in a lesson's taught-in figure.

**Book 3:** *Algebra and Trigonometry 2e* (pupil book) — OpenStax, Rice University, Houston, Texas; senior contributing author Jay Abramson (Arizona State University); © 2026 Rice University, CC BY-NC-SA 4.0; 116,281,063 bytes, 1,467 PDF pp. A college pupil textbook, filed at “Grade 13” in the library. Its Chapters 1–6 and 11–13 repeat *College Algebra 2e*'s teaching section for section (59 of its 79 sections; Chapters 1–6 keep the same pagination) — it is cited here for its own in-hand copy, and its own new teaching is the 20 trigonometry sections of Chapters 7–10. Printed pages are the book's own folios. PUPIL BOOK — its rows count in a lesson's taught-in figure.

## Turkmenistan added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Turkmenistan's whole filed in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **5 pupil files = 3 pupil works, plus 1 teacher guide** (6 documents, 840 PDF pages in all) — and the expansion map's "3 student + 0 teacher" kind split is **wrong** (**flag F1**: the map counts one file per grade/track slot; it misses the second parts of both Turkmen works and the teacher guide entirely — any plan sized on "3 books" under-sizes the read by about 2.5×). The pupil works are the complete Turkmen-medium Grade 1 and Grade 2 courses — *Matematika* Parts 1 and 2 for each grade (Atabaýew & Öwezowa, 3rd editions, 2019/2020) — and Part 1 only of the Russian-medium Grade 1 course (Rulieva, Chernikova, Mavlyanova & Satdykova, 4th ed. 2022), a separate work by a separate author team, not a translation of the Turkmen pair; the two Grade 1 Turkmen books are scans with no text layer and were read from page renders. The one teacher book, N. Açilowa's *Başlangyç synplarda matematikany okatmagyň usulyýeti* (Methodology of Teaching Mathematics in the Primary Classes, Ylym, 2015), is a teacher-training study guide: it teaches NO pupil content, so its rows are **teacher evidence only** and count in no lesson's taught-in figure; the five pupil books are real pupil textbooks, recommended by the Ministry of Education of Turkmenistan and served by the Ministry's own E-kitaphana, so their rows **count**. **Flag F2** (record-side: reported here, not fixed; merges never edit the records, and no `maths/books/turkmenistan.md` filing exists): a `books.json` note claims the Russian Grade 1 Part 2 "is E-kitaphana post 507" — post 507 is in fact **Grade 2 Russian Mathematics Part 1** (per the enumeration table and the grid match), no Grade 1 Russian Part 2 post exists in the catalogue, and that Part 2 is recorded nowhere as found; post 507 must not be double-counted as both a Grade 1 and a Grade 2 book, and the Russian Grade 1 work stands in hand as Part 1 only. **Flag F3**: the filing notes for these books rested on samples ("every-fifth-page verification", contents lists not yet verified); this full re-read confirms the sampled claims spot-checked — e.g. the kilogram section of Grade 1 (Turkmen) Part 2 does sit at printed pp. 63–64 — and supplies the missing contents lists for both Grade 2 parts (Part 1 contents at PDF p. 103; Part 2 at PDF pp. 118–119); no sampled content claim checked was found false. **Flag F4**: the map's "2 link-only" figure cannot be confirmed and looks far too low — the banked E-kitaphana grid match carries Maths links for the Grade 2 Russian pair and for Grades 3–9 Mathematics, Algebra and Geometry, plus a Grade 5 methodology guide; every one of those is link-only, not in hand and cited nowhere, and everything beyond Grade 2 is outside this set. Byte-identical duplicate posts are cited once, on the filed copy (post 403 = the Grade 1 Part 2 file; post 412 = the Grade 2 Part 1 file; post 418 = the Grade 2 Part 2 file; post 576 = the teacher guide's post 555 file), and every pupil file was pulled from its GitHub release asset at the exact recorded byte size (reconcile sweep #8 lists 23/23 Turkmenistan entries secured). Counting grain: one item per distinct teaching section of a book (its contents-list section, or a headed teaching block where the contents list runs broader), with consolidation/revision sections counted once per book-part; pure exercise pages inside a section are apparatus of that section's item. In every book in this set the printed folio equals the PDF page. One reading note, carried exactly: in the Grade 1 (Turkmen) Part 1 book's type, the empty answer box prints as a small square — in the PDF font layer it can appear as «*» (e.g. the comparing exercises "7 * 6" mean "put <, > or = between 7 and 6") — it is NOT a multiplication sign, and multiplication is not taught in that book. Not stocked as separate rows, exactly as the placements direct: the Grade 2 Part 1 money item 14 (manat notes and coins inside addition/subtraction stories at printed pp. 50, 61 — context only, no money section is taught in that book, and the placements count it inside items 11–13), and the four consolidation/revision items the placements assign to no lesson (Grade 2 Part 1 item 17, Grade 2 Part 2 item 42, Grade 1 Part 1 item 71, Grade 1 Russian Part 1 item 125) — practice over sections already stocked, no new content. Turkmenistan's class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED** on the as-filed set. Into this lesson the placements stock 3 items: 2 pupil + 1 teacher evidence.

**Book 1:** *Matematika (I kitap)* — Grade 1, Part 1 (Turkmen-medium pupil textbook) — M. Atabaýew & Z. Öwezowa, 3rd edition, Aşgabat: Türkmen döwlet neşirýat gullugy (Turkmen State Publishing Service), 2019; recommended by the Ministry of Education of Turkmenistan ("Türkmenistanyň Bilim ministrligi tarapyndan hödürlenildi"); Ministry E-kitaphana post 174; 62,590,372 bytes, 112 PDF pp. A scanned book with no text layer — read from page renders; grabbed from its GitHub release asset (mid-size-books-3) at the exact recorded byte size. Printed page = PDF page. PUPIL TEXTBOOK — its rows count in a lesson's taught-in figure.

**Book 2:** *Matematika (I kitap)* — Grade 2, Part 1 (Turkmen-medium pupil textbook) — M. Atabaýew & Z. Öwezowa, 3rd edition, Aşgabat: Türkmen döwlet neşirýat gullugy, 2020; recommended by the Ministry of Education of Turkmenistan; Ministry E-kitaphana post 527 (the byte-identical post 412 is the same file, cited once); 20,275,881 bytes, 104 PDF pp.; grabbed from its GitHub release asset (mid-size-books-8) at the exact recorded byte size. Printed page = PDF page. PUPIL TEXTBOOK — its rows count in a lesson's taught-in figure.

**Book 3:** *Başlangyç synplarda matematikany okatmagyň usulyýeti* [Methodology of Teaching Mathematics in the Primary Classes] (teacher-training study guide) — N. Açilowa, Aşgabat: Ylym, 2015; recommended by the Ministry of Education of Turkmenistan; a study-methodology guide for higher and secondary pedagogical schools; Ministry E-kitaphana post 555 (post 576 is the same file, cited once); 4,173,475 bytes, 272 PDF pp.; read from the copy held in the E-kitaphana work folder at the exact recorded byte size. ## Vanuatu added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Vanuatu's whole in-hand Maths set from the file — exactly 1 book, a teacher guide, and no pupil textbook: *Tija Gaed blong Matematiks Yia 3* (Mathematics Teacher Guide, Year 3, Bislama, MoET 2017). The expansion map is CORRECT for the in-hand set (0 student + 1 teacher = 1); the TSV's other distinct Vanuatu books are the Language & Communication Y2, Living in Community Y2 and Science Y3 teacher guides and the Bislama Spelling Dictionary Y2 — no Maths hides under another subject label and there is no "all subjects" Vanuatu volume in hand. The staging PDF was deleted after pushing (the pusher's normal pattern); the read copy was grabbed from the recorded source via its banked Wayback capture (2019-11-17 `id_` replay of education.gov.vu/docs/textbooks/Matematiks_2017.pdf) and is byte-identical to the record: 11,818,504 bytes, 317 PDF pages, SHA-256 2b7aa33d2ee0a81d9512e4f141c208bfc04a0abdf6d5bf77d58356a31cf22ec6, matching the staging `books.json` hash exactly. Printed folio = PDF page \u2212 1 throughout (verified on the outcomes section, weekly lesson footers and the glossary). Counting grain: one item per distinct teaching topic in the Year 3 column of the guide's Learning Outcomes & Indicators tables (printed pp. 12\u201322), worded from the outcome and its indicators and confirmed against the weekly lessons that teach it (Terms 1\u20133, printed pp. 23\u2013294); items taught in a weekly lesson but stated in no Year 3 outcome are marked (D). Assessment questions, resources lists and vocabulary boxes are apparatus for the counted items, not separate items. Record items R1\u2013R4 are stocked nowhere as teaching items: R1 the trilingual Bislama\u2013English\u2013French glossary of Year 3 mathematics words (printed pp. 313\u2013316) — teacher-reference apparatus; R2 the photocopiable appendix masters (printed pp. 295\u2013312) — they carry no teaching topic not already counted; R3 the programme facts from the introduction (printed pp. 4\u20136 — five strands, ten sub-strands, seven hours of Year 3 mathematics a week under the National Timetable Policy 2013); R4 the Beginning / Year 1 / Year 2 columns of the outcomes tables — those are the earlier years' guides, which are NOT in hand for Maths, and nothing from them is counted. The placements' named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the introduction apart from R3 (printed pp. 4\u201310 — lesson planning, literacy and inclusive-education guidance, teaching craft only); the Beginning/Year 1/Year 2 progression columns; the weekly vocabulary boxes, resources lists and assessment apparatus; and syllabus/timetable exposition carried as R3 only. Book errors E1\u2013E5 are carried taught corrected where they affect stocking: E1 — printed p. 15 abbreviates millilitres "(mm)" (mm is millimetres; millilitres are mL) and writes litres "(l)"; E2 — the Term 3 Week 3 length-conversion lesson (printed p. 233) is headed with the operations outcome NO.3.V1 though it is measurement; E3 — the money lesson (printed p. 202) cites its outcome as "M3.V1" for MM.3.V1; E4 — the Year 3 probability outcome code is printed "CPD.3.V1" (printed p. 21) against the CDP codes of the other columns; E5 — template slips in the term overviews (printed pp. 23, 210), none affecting content. Set boundary: the ministry `/docs/curriculum/` Maths Student's Books Y3\u2013Y6 (English stream), the French-stream Maths 3e\u20136e Ann\u00e9e, the Mathematics Teacher's Guide (Year 4) and the Kindergarten Guide (2013) are exact-URL candidates only — never downloaded, in no `books.json`, not in the push-state, not in hand and not read. Vanuatu is **TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE ONLY** in every lesson: every item is worded as what the guide teaches, Vanuatu is named in the countries line and in the rows below, and it is added to NO lesson's acceptance count, on the Ghana precedent. Vanuatu's class for Maths is **PARTIAL (evidence-only)**. Into this lesson the placements stock 1 teacher-evidence item(s) from Book 1.

**Book 1:** *Tija Gaed blong Matematiks Yia 3* (Mathematics Teacher Guide, Year 3, Bislama) \u2014 Vanuatu, Ministry of Education and Training (MoET, Ministri blong Edukesen mo Trening, Ripablik blong Vanuatu), 2017, developed with Australian/NZ aid support (Education Support Program); 11,818,504 bytes, 317 PDF pages, SHA-256 2b7aa33d2ee0a81d9512e4f141c208bfc04a0abdf6d5bf77d58356a31cf22ec6. Teacher guide \u2014 TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY. Printed folio = PDF page \u2212 1. Read in full: outcomes and indicators printed pp. 12\u201322; weekly lessons Term 1 printed pp. 23\u2013124, Term 2 printed pp. 125\u2013209, Term 3 printed pp. 210\u2013294.

| Lesson part | Where it comes from |
|---|---|
| Completing open number sentences | Vanuatu \u2014 *Tija Gaed blong Matematiks Yia 3* (teacher guide) \u2014 teacher evidence, printed p. 13 |

Vanuatu enters this lesson as teacher-guide evidence only: it is named in the countries line and in the rows above, but it is NOT added to the acceptance count above \u2014 Vanuatu's only in-hand Maths book is a teacher guide and no Vanuatu pupil textbook is in hand (the standing Ghana evidence precedent). Vanuatu's class for Maths is PARTIAL (evidence-only): the single in-hand book is read and stocked in full, and it advances only when a Vanuatu pupil textbook \u2014 the Student's Books Y3\u2013Y6 or the French-stream Maths 3e\u20136e Ann\u00e9e, today exact-URL candidates only \u2014 lands in hand and is read.
